vyāsakṛtam |
yasyā''jñayā jagatsraṣṭā virañciḥ pālako hāraḥ |
saṃhartā kālarudrākhyo namastasmai pinākine || 1 ||
munīnāmāśrayo nityaṃ naimiṣāraṇyamuttamam || 2 ||
śaunakādyā mahātmānaḥ śivabhaktā mahaujasaḥ |
dīrghasatraṃ prakurvantastatreśānasya tuṣṭaye || 3 ||
tasminsatre mahābhāgo munīnāṃ bhāgyagauravāt |
ṛṣaya ūcuḥ –
kathaṃ bhagavatā pūrvamādityenā''tmarūpiṇā |
purāṇaṃ kathitaṃ sauraṃ tanno vaktumihārhasi || 6 ||
kṛṣṇadvaipāyanātsākṣātpūrve hi viditaṃ tvayā |
tvatto nāsti paro vaktā purāṇānāṃ mahātapaḥ || 7 ||
santyanye bahavaḥ śiṣyā api tasya mahātmanaḥ ||
tathā'pei śiṣyavātsalyāttvaṃ purāṇeṣu yojitaḥ || 8 ||
yānyanyāni purāṇāni tvayoktāni mahāmune |
alaṃ taiḥ pārvatīkāntabhaktau bhaktiyutaṃ tvidam || 9 ||
na yajñairna tapobhirvā na dānairna taistathā |
śivabhaktimṛte yasmānmuktirnāstīti śuśruma || 10 ||
devo'yaṃ bhagavānbhānurantaryāmī sanātanaḥ |
yo brūte sarvavastūnāṃ tattvaṃ jñātvaiva nānyathā || 11 ||
ataḥ śraddhā hi mahatī śrotuṃ tvadvadanāmṛtam |
asmākaṃ vartate sūtaṃ romaharṣaṇa suvrata || 12 |
sūta uvāca -
trisatyaṃ trijagadyoniṃ trimārga ca tritattvam || 13 ||
yacchrutvā manujaḥ śīghraṃ pāpakañcukamutsṛjet || 14 ||
lokadvayaṃ paṭhedyastu lokamekamathāpi vā |
śraddhāvānpāpakarmā'pi sa gacchetsavituḥ padam || 15 ||
paurāṇī vṛttimāśritya ye jīvanti dvijātayaḥ |
tanmaṇḍalaṃ vinirbhidya tatsāyujyaṃ vrajanti te || 16 ||
vaktā yatra raviḥ sākṣācchrota tasya suto manuḥ |
māhātmyaṃ kathyate śaṃbharnāstyasmādadhikaṃ dvijāḥ || 17 ||
dvijāya
dhānāya śivaikātibuddhaye || 18 ||
āsīnmanuḥ sūryasuto vartate yo mahātapāḥ |
pratardanasya nṛpatairyajñe vipuladakṣiṇe |
tattvaṃ vicārayāmāsurmitho yatra maharṣayaḥ || 20 ||
aśaktāste mahābhāgā bhṛgvādyāstattvanirṇaye |
evaṃ sthiteṣu vipreṣu māyayā mohitātmasu || 21 ||
saṃśayāviṣṭacitteṣu vāgabhūdaśarīriṇī |
tapaḥ kurudhvaṃ viprendrāttapo'jñānanibarhaṇam || 22 ||
tapasā prāpyate sarvamiti te śuśruvurgiram |
śrutvā tu munayaḥ sarve bhṛgvādyā dagdhakilviṣāḥ || 23 ||
mainuṃ puraskṛtya yayuḥ kṣetraṃ vai dvādaśātmanaḥ |
viśrutaṃ dvādaśādityamiti lokeṣu tadvijāḥ || 24 ||
,
ete ca munayaḥ sarve tapasā dagdhakilviṣāḥ || 27 ||
paśyantu māṃ paraṃ devaṃ viśvāntaryāmiṇaṃ vibhum || 28 ||
sūta uvāca -
iti dṛṣṭvā raviṃ sākṣātpratyakṣaṃ purataḥ sthitam |
stutiṃ cakāra sa manurmunibhiḥ saha suvrataḥ || 30 ||
manuruvāca-
nama namo vareṇyāya varadāyāṃśumāline |
jyotirmaya namastubhyamanantāyājitāyaṃ te || 31 ||
trilokacakṣuṣe tubhyaṃ triguṇāyāmṛtāya ca |
namo dharmāya haṃsāya jagajjananahetave || 32 ||
naranārīśarīrāya namo mīdṛṣṭamāya te |
majñānāyākhileśāya saptāśvāya trimūrtaye || 33 ||
namo vyāhṛtirūpāya trilakṣāyā''śugāmine |
haryaśvāya namastubhyaṃ namo haritaṃvāhave || 34 ||
ekalakṣavilakṣāya bahulakṣāya daṇḍine |
tvāmṛte paramātmānaṃ na tatpaśyāmi daivatam || 37 ||
evaṃ stutvā manuḥ prāha bhagavantaṃ trayīmayam |
munibhiḥ saha dharmātmā samyagdarśanakāṅkṣibhiḥ || 38 ||
jñāte tasmiṃstu kiṃ rūpaṃ tasya jñānaṃ kimātmakam || 41 ||
caritaṃ tasya kiṃ tāta kiṃ tīrthaṃ tadadhiṣṭhitam |
keṣāmanugrahastasya tīrthe nivasatāṃ prabho || 42 ||
lakṣaṇaṃ ca purāṇānāṃ vratānāṃ ca kramo yathā |
śrāddhaṃ kathaṃ vā kriyate prāyaścittavidhiḥ katham |
bhānuruvāca -
purāṇe'sminmahābhāga sarvavedārthasaṃgrahe || 1 ||
tattvaṃ yadbhagavato rūpamīśasya śūlinaḥ |
viśvaṃ tenākhilaṃ vyāptaṃ nānyenetyabravīcchrutiḥ || 2 ||
sa evā''tmā samastānāṃ bhūtānāṃ manujādhipa |
caitanyarūpī bhagavānmahādevaḥ sahomayā || 3 ||
masti bahudhā bhāti līlayā kevalaḥ śivaḥ |
brahmaviṣṇvādirūpeṇa devadevo maheśvaraḥ || 4 ||
pṛṣṭo brahmādibhirdevaiḥ kastvaṃ deveti śaṃkaraḥ |
abravīdahamevaiko nānyaḥ kaściditi śrutiḥ || 5 ||
ātmabhūtānmahādevāllīlāvigraharūpiṇaḥ |
ādisarge samudbhūtau brahmaviṣṇū surottamau || 6 ||
prāhurbahuvidhaṃ tajjñā indraṃ mitramiti śrutiḥ || 7 ||
na tasmādadhikaḥ kaścinnāṇīyānapi kaścana |
mumukṣubhiḥ sadā dhyeyaḥ śiva eko nirañjanaḥ |
sarvamanyatparityajya mukta eva vimucyate || 9 ||
na tasya karmakārya vā bandhamuktī maheśituḥ |
ānandarūpayā gauryā krīḍati sma maheśvaraḥ || 10 ||
akṣeraṃ paramaṃ vyoma śaivaṃ jyotiranāmayam |
yastanna veda kiṃ vedairbrāhmaṇasya bhaviṣyati || 11 ||
+ nānyo vedyaḥ svayaṃjyotī rudra eko nirañjanaḥ |
tasmijjñāte'khilaṃ jñātamityāhurvedavādinaḥ || 12 ||
ahaṃ brahmā ca viṣṇuśva śakraścānye divaukasaḥ |
adyāpyupāyairvividhaiḥ śaṃbhodarśanakāṅkṣiṇaḥ || 13 ||
na dānairna tapobhirvā nāśvamedhādibhirmakhaiḥ |
bhaktyaivānanyayā rājañjñāyate bhagavāśivaḥ || 14 ||
yato vāco nivartante aprāpya manasā saha |
bhargādviśvasya bharaṇādviśvayonerumāpateḥ || 15 ||
tasya jñānamayī śaktiravyayā girijā śivā |
tayā saha mahādevaḥ sṛjatyavati hanti ca || 16 ||
abhedaḥ śivayoḥ siddho vahnidāhakayoriva || 17 ||
māyā sā paramā śaktirakṣairā girijā'vyayā |
māyāviśvātmako rudraratajjñātvā hyamṛtī bhavet || 18 ||
chasaṃjñitapustake na tasyetyādiślokātpūrva dharmārthakāmetyādi vedasiddhāntatyadhika dṛśyate |
tadyathā-
-
śivabhaktiḥ sadā satyaṃ nānyakiṃcana bhūtale || 1 ||
trilokyāṃ sukhakāmo yastena pūjyaḥ sadāśivaḥ |
śivabhaktiḥ mṛte saukhyaṃ kutaḥ syāt dehinām || 2 ||
śivabhaktyā dhanaṃ vidyā yaśaḥ śatranayastathā |
prāpyate vijayaḥ sarve satyametanna saṃśayaḥ || 3 ||
rogakṣayastathā''rogyaṃ yyaddhi manasecchati |
janastatsarvamāpnoti vedasya vacanaṃ yathā || 4 ||
tarhi sarve śivabhakti kathaṃ na kurvanti tathāss-
yadā lalāṭe dhātrā hi likhitaṃ saukhyamuttamam |
śivabhaktau tadā buddhijīyate nānyathā bhuvam || 5 ||
yadā lalāṭe sukhaprāptirlikhitā bhavetsadā śivasevāyāṃ matirbhavatīti vedasiddhāntaḥ |
svātmanyavasthitaṃ devaṃ viśvavyāpinamīśvaram |
bhaktyā paramayā rājañjñātvā pāśairvimucyate || 19 ||
saṃkalaṃ tasya bhāsaiva bhāti nānyena śaṃkaraḥ |
tasminprakāśamāne hi naiva bhāntyanalādayaḥ || 20 ||
tasminmaheśvare gūḍhe vidyāvidye kṣarākṣare |
vidhātari jagannāthe viśvaṃ bhāti na vastutaḥ || 21 ||
tasminmaheśvare viśvamotaṃ prorta na saṃśayaḥ |
tasmiñjñāte'khilaiḥ pāśairmucyate manujeśvaraḥ || 22 ||
brahmaviṣṇvādayo devā munayo manavastathā |
sa evaiko na cāneko na dvirūpaḥ kadācana |
tasyā''jñayā'khilaṃ viśva vartate tanniyantritam || 24 ||
dakṣiṇāṅgādvirūpākṣaḥ, sṛṣṭyarthe līlayā kila || 25 ||
tasmai vedānpurāṇāni dattavānagrajanmane |
vāsudevaṃ jagadyoniṃ sattvodriktaṃ sanātanam || 26 ||
asṛjatpālanārthaṃ ca vāmabhāgānmaheśvaraḥ |
hṛdayātkāla rudrākhyaṃ jagasaṃhārakārakam || 27 ||
asṛjadyogināṃ dhyeyo nirguṇastu svayaṃ śivaḥ |
viśvaṃ tasmāddhi saṃbhūtaṃ tasmistiṣṭhati śaṃkare || 28||
layameṣyati tatraiva trayametatsvalīlayā |
jñānene bhaktiyuktena jñātavyaḥ parameśvaraḥ |
na paśyāmi mahādevādadhikaṃ devatāntaram || 30 ||
vedā api tamevārthamāhuḥ svāyaṃbhuve'ntare ||
vedā ūcu:-yaṃ prapaśyanti vidvāṃso yoginaḥ kṣapitāśayāḥ |
niyamya. karaṇagrāmaṃ sa evā''tmā maheśvaraḥ || 31 ||
brahmaviṣvindracandrādyā yasya devasya kiṃkarāḥ |
adyāpi na vayaṃ vidmaḥ sa devastripurāntakaḥ || 33 ||
śṛṇvantu devatāḥ sarvāḥ satyamasmadvacaḥ param |
nāsti rudrānmahādevādadhikaṃ daivataṃ param || 34 ||
na yathā kūrmaromāṇi śṛṅgaṃ na śaśamastake |
na yathā'sti viyatpuṣpaṃ tathā nāsti harātparam || 35 ||
śivabhaktimṛte yastu sukhamāptumihecchati |
ajāgalastanādeva sa dugdhaṃ pātumicchati || 36 ||
mahādevaṃ vijānīyādehamasmīti paṇḍitaḥ |
anyatkimasmādapyasti jñātavyaṃ muktihetave || 37 ||
brāhmī nārāyaṇī raudrī pū̃jayitvā maheśvarīm |
yatprapaśyanti yogīndrāttadvidyācchāṃkaraṃ padam || 38 ||
kramāccatrāṇi caṃkramya śaṅkhinyāmupari sthitam |
yadabhivyajyate jyotistadvidyācchākaraṃ padam || 39 ||
devayānapathaṃ hitvā pitṛyāṇaṃ tathottaram |
gaganādyo ravaḥ sūkṣmaḥ śaṃkarasya sa vācakaḥ || 40 ||
vālāgramātraṃ hṛtpadme sthitaṃ devamumāpatim |
ye'nupaśyanti vidvāṃsara teṣāṃ śāntirhi śāśvatī || 42 ||
pṛthivyāṃ tiṣṭhati vibhuḥ pṛthivī vetti naiva tam |
apsu tiṣṭhati naivāsspastaṃ viduḥ parameśvaram |
āpo rūpaṃ ca yasyaiva namastaramai jalātmane || 44 ||
yosar tiṣṭhatyameyātmā na taṃ vetti kadācana |
abhī rūpaṃ bhavedyasya tasmai vahnātmane namaḥ || 45 ||
tiṣṭhatyajastraṃ yo vāyau na vāyurvetti taṃ param |
vāyuryasya bhavedrūpaṃ tasmai vāyvātmane namaḥ || 46 ||
vyoni tiṣṭhati yo nityaṃ vyoma vetti na taṃ haram |
vyoma yasya bhavedrūpaṃ tasmai vyomātmane namaḥ || 47 ||
sūrye tiṣṭhati yo devo na sūryo vetti śaṃkaram |
yasya sūryo bhavedrūpaṃ taramai sūryātmane namaḥ || 48 ||
yaścandre tiṣṭhati virbhuścandro vetti na śāśvatam |
candro yasya bhavedrūpaṃ taramai candrātmane namaḥ || 49 ||
yajamāne tiṣṭhati yo na taṃ vetti kadācana |
yajamāno'pi yadrūpaṃ yajamānātmane namaḥ || 50 ||
tvatto vayaṃ samudbhūtāstvayyeva vilayastathā |
pramāṇapadamārūḍhāstvatprasādādvṛṣadhvaja || 51 ||
bhānuruvāca -
pratyakṣaḥ samabhūtteṣāṃ vedānāṃ manujādhipa || 52 ||
sahasraśīrṣā puruṣaḥ sūrya somāgnilocanaḥ || 53 ||
vedānuvāca bhagavāndevadevo maheśvaraḥ || 54 ||
īśvara uvāca -
matprasādādbhaviṣyadhvaṃ he vedā lokapūjitāḥ |
yuṣmānāśritya viprendrāḥ karma kurvanti nānyathā || 55 ||
ye yuṣmānsamatikramya yatkicitkarma kurvate |
niṣphalaṃ tadbhavetkarma teṣāṃ yuṣmadaivajñayā || 56 |
yuṣmadvaco nānyaditi matvā dhīro na śocati || 57 ||
ye vai yuṣmānanādṛtya śāstraṃ kurvanti mānavāḥ |
niraye te vipacyante yāvadindrāścaturdaśa || 58 ||
śreyase triṣu lokeṣu na vedādadhikaṃ param |
vidyate nātra saṃdeha iti datto varo mayā || 59 ||
bhānuruvāca -
- yadetadaiśvaraṃ tejaḥ sarvagaṃ bhāti kevalam |
tadeva śaraṇaṃ gaccha yadīcchasi paraṃ padam || 1 ||
tadeva sarvabhūtasthaṃ cinmātraṃ tamasaḥ param |
pratyakṣaṃ sarvabhūtānāmajñānāṃ tadviparyayaḥ |
viśvamāyāvidhātāraṃ diraṣṭādaśarūpaṇam || 3 ||
bhaktigrādyaṃ mahādevaṃ jānīyātmani saṃsthitam |
ātmabhūte mahādeve yogidhyeye sanātane || 4 ||
bhaktimāsthāya paramoṃ paraṃ nirvāṇamāpnuhi |
tīrthayātrā bahuvidhā yajñāśca vividhāḥ kṛtāḥ || 5 ||
yeṣāṃ janmasahasreṣu teṣāṃ bhaktirbhavecchive |
akṣayaḥ paramo dharmo bhaktileśena jāyate || 6 ||
nāsti tasmātparo dharma ityāhurvedavādinaḥ |
dharmo bahuvidhaḥ prokto munibhistattvadarśibhiḥ || 7 ||
yajñāttīrthājjapādānāddharmaḥ syādbahusādhanaḥ || 8 ||
sādhanaprārthanākleśaḥ parasaṃpattiduḥkhadaḥ |
yaḥ punaḥ śivadharmastu na sādhanamapekṣate || 9 ||
saṃcitaṃ janmasāhasraiḥ pāpaṃ rūpamaṃ yadi |
karoti bhasmasocchaktiḥ śaṃbhoramitatejasaḥ || 10 ||
kurvannapi sadā pāpaṃ sakṛdevārcayecchvim |
lipyate na sa pāpena yāti māheśvaraṃ padam || 11 ||
ye smaranti mahādevaṃ yadi pāparatā api |
te vijñeyā mahātmāna iti satyaṃ bravīmyaham || 12 ||
pādmakalpa samudbhūtāṃ brahmaṇā samudīritām || 14 ||
vakṣye'haṃ taṃ praṇamyāssdāvīśaṃ bhuvananāyakam || 15 ||
āsīdādye kṛtayuge saptadvīpaikarāvalī |
indradyumna iti khyāto rājā paramadhārmikaḥ || 16 ||
tasya putro mahābhāgaḥ sudyumna iti viśrutaḥ |
aiśvaryairakhilairbhāti yathā divi śacīpatiḥ || 17||
pratiṣṭhānapure ramye gaṅgātīre manorame |
tatra sthitvā'khilāṃ pṛthvīṃ tasminrājani śāsati || 18 ||
kadācittatra bhagavāṃstṛṇabindurmahāmuniḥ |
ājagāma sa taṃ draṃ sudyumnaṃ priyadarśanam || 19 ||
tamāyāntaṃ muniṃ dṛṣṭvā rājā rudrārcane rataḥ |
udvāsyāca mahābāhurutthāya ca kṛtāñjaliḥ || 20 ||
yathāvadabhivādyātha dadāvāsanamuttamam |
yathāvanmadhuparkādi tasmai sarva nyavedayat || 21 ||
adya dhanyaḥ kṛtārtho'smi saphalaṃ jīvitaṃ mama |
bhagavānāgato yasmānmāṃ draṣṭuṃ munisattama || 22 ||
kimarthamāgato brahmankṛtakṛtyo'smi suvrata |
viśeṣācchaṃkare bhakto na durlabhamihāsti te || 23 ||
bhānuruvāca -
śivabhaktyamṛtāsvādaparānandaikanirbharaḥ || 24 ||
tṛṇabinduruvāca -
rājanyaduktaṃ bhavatā tattathaiva na saṃśayaḥ |
tuṃ samāgato rājañjanmanastava gauravam |
jānāti bhagavāneko viśvavyāpī maheśvaraḥ || 32 ||
idaṃ puṇyamiti jñātvā kṛtaṃ nānena yadyapi |

pāpaleśo'pi nāsyāsti iti me niścitā matiḥ || 34 ||
sudyumna uvāca -
tasya tadvacanaṃ śrutvā citraguptasya dhīmataḥ |
etasminnantare tatra vimānaṃ sārvakāmikam |

tasminyuge bhaviṣyanti brāhmaṇāḥ śūdrayājakāḥ || 18 ||
svādhyāyaṃ na kariṣyanti brāhmaṇā hi kalau yuge || 19 ||
na paṭhanti tathā vedāñśreyase brāhmaṇādhamāḥ |
pratigrahārthaṃ vedāṃśca paṭhiṣyanti kalau yuge || 20 ||
puruṣottamamāśritya śivanindāratā dvijāḥ |
kalau yuge bhaviṣyanti teṣāṃ trātā na mādhavaḥ || 21 ||
svāṃ svāṃ vṛttiṃ parityajya paravṛttyupajīvakāḥ |
etānpāparatāndṛṣṭvā rājānaśvāvicārakāḥ |
bhaviṣyanti kalau prāpte vṛthā jātyabhimāninaḥ || 23 ||
uccāsanagatāḥ śūdrā dṛṣṭvā ca brāhmaṇāṃstadā |
na calantyalpamatayaḥ saṃprāpte tu kalau yuge || 24 ||

+ tāḍayanti durātmāno brāhmaṇānrājasevakāḥ |
na nivārayate rājā tāndṛṣṭvā'pi kalau yuge || 28 ||
evaṃ ghore kaliyuge kiṃ tacchreyaskaraṃ dvija |

sanatkumāra uvāca -
na tatra yugadharmo'sti naiva lagnā vasuṃdharā || 1 ||

sūta uvāca -
yaduktaṃ bhānunā pūrva manave munisattamāḥ |

ratneśvare tadā jyotirdarśanānmanujottamaḥ |
ratneśvaraṃ tu saṃpūjya pārāśaryo mahāmuniḥ || 28 ||
draṣṭuṃ devādhideveśaṃ vṛddhakāleśvaraṃ yayau |
tasmĩlliṅga mahādevaḥ sadā tiṣṭhati līlayā || 29 ||
anugrahāya lokānāmayā saha viśvabhuk |
pṛthivyāṃ yāni liṅgāni santi divyāni vai dvijāḥ || 30 ||
vṛddhakāleśvare dṛṣṭe dṛṣṭānyeva na saṃśayaḥ |
devasya pūrvadigbhāge kūpoṃ muniniṣevitaḥ || 31 ||
pūritaḥ puṇyasalilairdevadevena śaṃbhunā |

sūta uvāca -
dra dakṣeśvaraṃ devaṃ bhaktānāṃ siddhidāyakam || 1 ||

ārādhya devadeveśaṃ bahūnyabdaśatāni vai |
tasya prasanno bhagavāndevadevaḥ sahomayā || 3 ||
vairaṃ nidhāya manasi śaṃbhunā saha suvratāḥ |
dakṣaḥ prācetaso yajñamakarojjāhnavītaṭe || 10 ||
tasminyajñe samāhūtā indrādyā devatāgaṇāḥ |
brahmā ca viṣṇunā sārdhamāhūtastena dhīmatā |
devānsarvāṃśca bhāgārthamahūtānpadmasaṃbhavaḥ || 12 ||
dṛṣṭvā śivena rahitāndakṣaṃ pratyevamabravīt |
brahmovāca -
aho dakṣa mahāmūṃḍha durbuddhe kiṃ kṛtaṃ tvayā |
devāḥ sarve samāhūtāḥ śaṃkareṇa vinā katham || 13 ||
antaryāmī sa viśveśaḥ sarveṣāmeva dehinām |

yasya pādarajaḥsparśādbrahmatvaṃ prāptavānaham |

sūta uvāca -

vyāsa uvāca --

ṛṣaya ūcuḥ -
kiṃ lakṣaṇaṃ purāṇānāṃ teṣāṃ dānena kiṃ phalam |
tataḥ śrāddhavidhānaṃ ca prāyaścittaṃ kathaṃ bhavet || 2 ||
sarvametadaśeṣeṇa sūta no vaktumarhasi |
bhāgadvayena kathitaṃ brahmāṇḍamati saṃjñitam |
idaṃ brahmapurāṇasya khilaṃ sauramanuttamam |
iyaṃ puṇyatamā khyātā saṃhitā pāpanāśinī || 14 ||
vaivasvatāya manave kathitā raviṇā purā |
dānamasya purāṇasya dānānāmuttamaṃ dvijāḥ || 15 ||
yo dadyācchivabhaktāya brāhmaṇāya tapasvine |

sarvapāpavinirmukto brahmaloke mahīyate |

skanda uvāca-
bhūtakāryayidaṃ dehamāpadrogākulaṃ param |
viṣayaḥ pīḍyate deva sukha duḥkhātmakaiḥ sadā || 1 ||
abhibhūto yadā yogī duḥkhairadhyātmasaṃbhavaiḥ |
kimupāyaṃ tadā tasya yadā vai bhautikasya ca || 2 ||
yātanāyopasargāṇāṃ prasādādyogināṃ vada || 3 ||
īśvara uvāca -
sāttvikā rājasā vighnāstāmasāstviha yoginām |
yogatrākarāḥ sarve bhavanti bhavatāmapi || 4 ||
'upasargā bhavantyete sāttvikāstu ṣaḍeva hi || 5 ||
daridro'hamahaṃ cā''ḍhyaḥ śūro'haṃ durbalastathā |
mūrkho'haṃ ca suvidvāṃśca surūpo'hamarūpavān || 6 ||
dātā'haṃ kṛpaṇaścāhaṃ sukhī bhogyahameva ca |
akulīnaḥ kulīnaśca kaṇṭakaḥ kaṇṭakojjhitaḥ || 7||
madīyaṃ sarvametaddhi vastvityādiprajalpanam |
ahaṃkāramayaṃ kiṃcidyattatkṛtsnaṃ hi rājasam || 8 ||
andhatvaṃ caiva bādhirya paṅgatvaṃ duṣṭarogatā |
śirorogo jvaraḥ śūlaiyakṣmamūrchā bhramādayaḥ || 9 ||
rājasāstāmasāḥ sarve tamohaṃkārasaṃyutāḥ |
vyādhayo miśrabhāvena pīḍayantīha dehinam || 10 ||
kevalaṃ jāḍyabhāvena mūḍhatvaṃ mohana tathā |
ajñānatvaṃ ca mūkatvamityādyāstāmasāḥ smṛtāḥ || 11 ||
gulakā yātudhānāca kiṃnaroragarākṣasāḥ |
devadānava raudrāśca daityā mātarajā gaṇāḥ || 12 ||
tāmasāstu grahā bhūtā vāyubhūtā naraṃ sadā |
pīḍayantīha vighnā hi yogābhyāsarataṃ grahaiḥ || 13 ||
evamādyupasargāṇāṃ vāraṇāya ca dhāraṇām |
vakṣyāmi vividhāṃ vatsa yogināṃ siddhihetave || 14 ||
tvagādisaptadhātūnāmekībhūtaṃ vicintayet |
praṇavaṃ kaṇṭhanāsāgre sabījaṃ vahnidīpitam || 15 ||
vāruṇeṣu ca sarveṣu upasargeṣu yogavit |
etadeva carennityamupasargādayo yayuḥ || 16 ||
pittarogābhibhūto vā yogī yogaparāyaṇaḥ |
suvṛttaṃ codunāthasya cākṣaraṃ tatra cintayet |
sudhābhilaṣitaṃ dhyāyetsvasya mūrdhni śina-dam || 18 ||
praviśya brahmarūpeṇa deha nirvāṇajaṃ smaret |
śītalena sugandhena hattattvaṃ cāpi tena ve || 19 ||
paittikāśropasargāya mānunā timiraṃ yathā |
viṣajvarajarādyāśca naśyantyabhyāsato dhruvam || 20 ||
+ nāśayedandhatāṃ yogī divyadṛṣṭiḥ prajāyate |
kṣipyāmānamanyaṃ ca candradaivatyayā pibet || 21 ||
pītvā pārthivataśvena svambhaṃ vāyorvināśayet |
puṣṭirevātulā tasya sthirasvaṃ rujahīnatā || 22 |
hṛttattvaṃ ca supītābhamamaratvaṃ tathā smaran |
śrotramākāśavāyvośca atraikatvaṃ vicintayet || 23 ||
mocayetaṃ punarvāyuṃ badhiratvavināśanam |
śṛṇoti dūrataḥ sarva śrutaghārī bhavetsadā || 24 ||
viyanmayo'tha saṃcārī satatābhyāsayogataḥ |
saroja rasanāyāṃ ca taddraṣṭāraṃ sakarṇikam || 25 ||
smṛtvā madhye punardhyācchuklavarṇī svarasvatīm |
ased ca śirorogaṃ mukharogānvināśayet || 26||
prajñā caivaṃ smṛtirmedhā kavitvaṃ buddhiruttamā |
stambhanaṃ duṣṭatvānāṃ sarvavāyuñjayetsadā || 27 ||
hṛtsarojagataṃ devamaṣṭādaśabhujairyutam |
nīlāruṇaṃ mahākārya kcindrajaṭādharam || 28 ||
bhujaṅgamhārābharaṇaṃ sarvakaṅkanūpuram || 29 ||
jvālāmālākulaṃ dīsaṃ bhābhāsitadigānanam |
abhedyaṃ vijayaṃ raudramobhyaṃ tridaśeśvaram || 30 ||
tayathā-vṛṣṭama tre tu tasminnai maneneotyāpayetkṣaṇāt |
lakṣamādhāya tatraiva yojayenmāye ṣaṇmukha |
ghṛtaṃ ghṛteṣveva yatha niyuktaṃ prayāti caikyādaviśeṣabhāvam |
tathaiva līno na bhavetsa bhūyaḥ pare caturthe tvanayā ca yuktyā |
sūta uvāca -
kamasaśita pustakeṣu ya ekādaśo'dhyāyaḥ sa evātra saṃgṛhītaḥ |
kṛṣṇāṣṭamīvratānnānyadvatamasti vibhūtidam |
kṛṣṇāṣṭamītrataṃ kṛtvā brahmā brahmatvamāpnuyāt || 2 ||
viṣṇutvaṃ prāptavānviṣṇuḥ sureśatvaṃ śacīpatiḥ |
kubero yakṣarājatvaṃ niyantṛtvaṃ yamaḥ svayam || 3 ||
candraścandratvamāpanno gaṇeśatvaṃ gaṇādhipaḥ |
rakandaḥ senāpatitvaṃ ca tathā cānye gaṇeśvarāḥ || 4 ||
kṛtvā caiśvaryamāpannāḥ saubhāgyaṃ devavallabhāḥ |
yayātiḥ sārvabhaumatvaṃ tathā cānye nṛpottamāḥ |
ṛṣayo munayaḥ siddhā gandharvāṇāṃ ca kanyakāḥ || 6 ||
kṛtvā caiva parāṃ siddhiṃ prāptāśca munipuṅgavāḥ |
nandīśvareṇa yatproktaṃ nāradāya mahātmane || 7 kṛṣṇāṣṭamīvrataṃ śreṣṭhaṃ sarvakāmaphalapradam |
tatra nandīśvaraṃ dṛṣṭvā sarvajñaṃ śaṃbhuvallabham |
sarvānugrahakartāraṃ stutvā tu vividhaiḥ stavaiḥ |
abravītpraṇipatyātha daṇḍavannārado muniḥ || 10 ||
nārada uvāca -
bhagavansarvatattvajña sarveṣāmabhayaprada |
kena tena cīrṇena tapovṛttiḥ prajāyate || 11 ||
saubhāgyaṃ kāntimaiśvaryamapatyaṃ ca yaśastathā |
śāśvatīṃ muktimante ca paśupāśavimocanīm || 12 ||
bhagavaṃstadvrataṃ brūhi kāruṇyācchaṃkarabhiyam |
nandikeśvara uvāca –
kṛṣṇāṣṭamīvrataṃ śreṣṭhamasti devaṛṣe śṛṇu || gaṇeśatvaṃ mayā labdhaṃ yena cīrṇena nārada || 13 ||
māse mārgaśire prāpte kṛṣṇāṣṭamyāṃ jitendriyaḥ |
aśvatthadantakāṣṭhena kṛtvā vai dantadhāvanam || 14 ||
āgatya bhavanaṃ paścātpūjayecchaṃkaraṃ prabhum || 15 ||
gomūtraṃ prāśya vidhivadupavāsī bhavenniśi |
pūjayecchaṃ bhunāmānaṃ bhagavantaṃ maheśvaram || 17 ||
vājapeyāṣṭakaphalaṃ prāpnoti śraddhayā'nvitaḥ |
māghe vaṭasya kathitaṃ gokṣīraṃ prāśanaṃ smṛtam || 18 ||
māheśvaraṃ susaṃpūjya gomedhasyāṣṭakaṃ phalam |
phālgune ca tadevoktaṃ kāryaṃ vai prāśanaṃ ca tat || 19 ||
saṃpūjayenmahādevaṃ rājasūyāṣṭakaṃ phalam |
kāṣṭhamaudumbaraṃ caitre prāśane varjitā janāḥ || 20 it pūjayetsthāṇunāmānamaśvamedhaphalaṃ labhet |
śivaṃ saṃpūjya vaiśākhe pītvā caiva kuśodakam || 21 ||
naramedhāṣṭaphalaṃ prāpnotyeva hi nārada |
jyeṣṭhe lākṣaṃ bhavetkāṣṭhaṃ pūjyaḥ paśupatirvibhuḥ || 22 ||
gavāṃ śṛṅgodakaṃ prāśya svapedevasya saṃnidhau |
pālāśaṃ śrāvaṇe proktaṃ śarteṃ saṃpūjya nārada |
prāśayitvā'rkapatrāṇi kalpaṃ śivapure vaset || 25 ||
āśvine jambuvṛkṣasya dantakāṣṭhamudīritam |
īśvaraṃ pūjayedbhaktyā prāśayettaṇḍulodakam || 27 ||
pāyasaṃ madhusaṃyuktaṃ ghṛtena supariplutam |
devāya dadyādadhyannaṃ vitānadhvajacāmaram |
saratnāṃ tāmrakalaśīṃ gāmalaṃkṛtya nārada |
kṛṣṇāṣṭamīvrataṃ samyakprāptaṃ devaṛṣe mayā || 33 ||
yaduktaṃ devadevena devyai viśvasṛjā purā || 34 ||
sūta uvāca -
evaṃ nandīśvarācchrutvā nārado munipuṅgavāḥ |
kṛṣṇāṣṭamīvrataṃ puṇyaṃ yayau badarikāśramam || 35 ||
atisatrasya yajñasya phalaṃ prānotyanuttamam || 36 ||
sūta uvāca -
anyadvataṃ pāpaharaṃ devadevasya cakriṇaḥ |
yaduktaṃ bhānunā pūrva yājñavalkyāya yogine || 1 ||
yājñavalkya uvāca -
tasyāṃ viśiṣṭaṃ yatpuṇyaṃ vada kaśyapanandana || 2||
sūrya uvāca -
dvādaśī viṣṇudayitā dvādaśī vaiṣṇavī tithiḥ |
śravaṇena samāyuktā kadācidyadi labhyate || 3||
yā tu puṣyeṇa saṃyuktā phālgunasya sitā tu vai |
kṛtārtho jāyate martyastāmupoṣya dvijottama |
saṃpūjya vastrapuṣpādyaiḥ phalaṃ sāyaṃ samaśnute |
ekaṃ jatrā sahasrasya japtasyā''noti vai phalam || 7||
dānaṃ sahasraguṇitaṃ tathā vai viprabhojanam |
homaścaivopavāsaśca sahasrasya phalapradaḥ || 8 ||
ṛcamekāmadhīte yo vimaḥ śraddhāsamanvitaḥ |
ṛgvedasya samagrasya sadaiva phalamaśrute || 9 ||
tanāśayati govindaratarayāmabhyarcya yatnataḥ || 10 ||
sarvapāpavinirmukto viṣṇuloke mahīyate || 11 ||
yaḥ kṛtvā dvādaśīmimāṃ kṣapayedbhaktimānnaraḥ |
brahmaṇo divasaṃ yāvattāvatsvarge mahīyate || 12 ||
tasmindine tu saṃprāpte yatkartavyaṃ bravīmyaham |
gandhapuṣpopahāraiśca vividhairvidhivannaraḥ |
matsyā pādau prathamaṃ kūrmāya ca tathā kaṭim || 14 ||
varāhāyeti jaṭharaṃ narasiṃhāya vā uraḥ |
vāmanāyeti vai kaṇṭhaṃ bhujaṃ rāmadvayeti ca || 15 |
yajedrāmeti ca mukhaṃ pradyumnāyeti nāsikām |
kṛṣṇanāmnā ca netre dve buddhanāmnā tathā śiraḥ || 16 ||
aferral art haānvāmaneti ca sarvataḥ |
bhaktyā cāssrādhya govindaṃ gopālaṃ ca tathā niśi || 17 ||
tatastasyāgrataḥ śuddhaṃ nyasetkṛṣṇājinaṃ budhaḥ |
tasyopari tilānāṃ tu kṛṣṇānāmāḍhakaṃ nyaset || 18 ||
madhyataḥ prasthamekaṃ tu daridraḥ kuḍavaṃ tathā |
tilālābhe yavāḥ kāryā godhūmāratadalābhataḥ || 19 ||
sukhaṃ tatra phalaṃ brahmastilaiḥ mābhoti mānavaḥ |
pracchādya pātraṃ vāsobhirahataiḥ suparīkṣitaiḥ |
yathāśaktyā kṛtaṃ haravaṃ kṛtayajñopavītinam |
evaṃrūpaṃ tu taṃ kṛtvā vāmanaṃ bhaktimānnaraḥ || 22 ||
sthāpayettantupātrasthaṃ bhaktyā samyagupoṣitaḥ |
puṣpairgandhaiḥ phalairdhūpaiḥ kālotthairarcayeddharim || 23 ||.
aitairmanapadairdevaṃ naivedyaiśva prapūjayet || 25 ||
bhaktasyātra viśeṣeṇa phalaṃ koṭiguṇottaram |
tatastasya samīpe tu dadhibhaktaṃ ghaṭe nyaset || 26 ||
karakaṃ vāripūrṇa ca sugandhadravyasaṃyutam |
chatraṃ caivākṣasūtraṃ ca pāduke guḍikāṃ tathā || 27 ||
evaṃ saṃpūjya vidhivaddevadevaṃ janārdanam |
evaṃ sarvarajanyante prabhāte vimale sati |
gurau ca sati nānyasmai dātavyamiti niścitam || 30 ||
vedādhyetre samaṃ dānaṃ dviguṇaṃ tadvide tathā |
ācārye dānamekaṃ ca sahastraguṇitaṃ tathā || 31 ||
gurau sati tato'nyasya vrataṃ yaśca nivedayet |
sa durgatimavāprati dattaṃ bhavati niṣphalam || 32 ||
avidyo vā savidyo vā gurureva janārdanaḥ |
mārgastho vā vimārgastho gurureva sadā gatiḥ || 33 ||
pratipannaṃ guruṃ yaiśva mohādvipratipadyate |
sa janmakoṭiṃ narake pacyate puruṣādhamaḥ || 34 ||
mantreṇānena dātavyaṃ purāṇapaṭhitena ca || 35 ||
mantreṇa pratigṛhṇīyādbrāhmaṇaśca dvijottama |
vāmano buddhido dātā dravyastho vāmanaḥ svayam || 36 ||
iti pratigrahamantraḥ |
anirvāyuryamaścaiva pāpaṃ haratu me sadā || 38 ||
ityannadānamantraḥ |
parjanyo varuṇaḥ sūryaḥ salilaṃ keśavaḥ śivaḥ |
svaṣṭā yamo vaiśravaṇaḥ pāpaṃ haratu me sadā ||39||
iti saliladānamanyaḥ |
pṛṣadājyaṃ ca saṃprāśya paścādbhuñjīta vāgyataḥ || 40 ||
samāpite vrate tasminbrahmaśṛṇu ca yatphalam || 41 ||
brahmaṇaḥ pralayaṃ yāvattāvatsvarge mahīyate |
punaḥ svargādbhuvaṃ prāpya jāyate mahatāṃ kule |
sarvānkāmānavāpnoti tato muktiṃ ca gacchati |
indrasyāvarajo devo ramāhṛdayanandanaḥ || 44 ||
balirvaddhastvayā deva gṛhāṇārghyaṃ tu vāmana || ityarghyamantraḥ |
sūta uvāca -
purā devena rudreṇa dagdhaḥ kāmo durāsadaḥ |
upoṣitā tithistena tenānaṅgatrayodaśī || 3 ||
bhaktyā tvananyayā devaṃ pūjayecchaśiśekharam |
puṣpairnānāvidhairdhūpairnaivedyaiśva phalaistathā || 5
śaṃbhunānā tilaihoṃmaṃ kuryādaṣṭottaraṃ śatam |
yogeśvaraṃ susaṃpūjya pauṣe prāśrīta candanam || 7 ||
nāṭeśvaraṃ susaṃpūjya māghamāse jitendriyaḥ || 8 ||
mauktikaṃ prāśya viprendrāḥ phalaṃ tasya vadāmyaham |
bahusvarṇasya yajñasya phalaṃ śataguṇaṃ bhavet || 9 ||
saṃpūjya phālgune vīraṃ kaḍūnelaṃ prāśayonārī |
gomedhasya phalaṃ prāpya modate devarāḍiva || 10 ||
surūpaṃ nāmaṃ vai caitre citraratnavinirmitam |
karpūraṃ prāśayedrātrau naramedhaphalaṃ labhet || 11 ||
vaiśākhe ca mahārūpaṃ deveśaṃ ca prapūjayet |
jyeṣṭhe pradyumnanāmānaṃ lavaṅgaṃ prāśayenniśi |
vājapeyasya yajñasya phalamaṣṭaguṇottaram || 13 ||
umābhartetināmānamāṣāḍhe saṃprapūjayet |
pūjayecchrāvaṇe śūlapāṇinaṃ parameśvaram |
prāśayedgandhatoyaṃ tu abhiṣṭomaphalaṃ labhet || 15 ||
māse bhādrapade vimāḥ sadyojātaṃ prapūjayet |
māse cāśvayuje prāpte tridaśādhipatiṃ yajet |
svarṇodakaṃ tu saṃprāśya svarṇakoṭiphalaṃ labhet || 17 ||
viśveśvaraṃ ca kārtikyāṃ pūjayedbhaktisaṃyutaḥ |
madanaraya phalaṃ prāśya kāmavatdyutimānbhavet || 18 ||
pratimāsaṃ pravakṣyāmi dantakāṣṭhāni vai dvijāḥ |
mallikā khādiraṃ caiva lakṣāpāmārgajaṃ tathā || 19||
jambudumbarajāśvatthaṃ mālatī vaṭajaṃ tathā |
mālatyāḥ prathamaṃ tāvattato marutrakaṃ tathā || 21 ||
karavīraṃ tathā kundamarkapatrāṇi suvratāḥ |
tato mandārapuṣpāṇi mallikākusumāni ca || 22 ||
kādambaṃ yūthikāpuṣpaṃ dhatturaṃ śatapatrakam |
durvāṅkurāṇi deyāni naivedyāni yathākramam || 23 ||
odanaṃ kṛśaraṃ caina śarkarāmodakāstathā |
kaṃsāraṃ yāvakāstatra tataḥ sauhālikā bhavet || 24 ||
pañcakhādyaṃ paraṃ proktaṃ ghṛtapūramanantaram |
śālibhaktena naivedyaṃ guṇakāratadanantaram || 25 ||
nānāvidhānaṃ naivedyaṃ kārtikyāṃ parikalpayet |
śaṃkarāya namaḥ pādau gauye gulphe śivāya ca |
śivāyai jānunī pūjya śaṃbhavāyodbhavāya ca || 27 ||
kaiṭiṃ manmathanāśāya madanāyai surerśvare |
nābhi bhavāya saṃpūjya bhavānyai nama ityumām || 28 ||
vakṣo devādhidevāya aparṇāyai namaḥ śivām |
ratanau viśveśvarāyeti surakāntyai namo namaḥ || 29 ||
kaṇṭha bhīmograrūpāya girijāyai namaḥ śivām |
skandhaṃ tridaśavandyāya triśūlinyai namaḥ śivām || 35||
bāhū dhūrjayetyuktvā dhūsarāyai namaḥ śivām |
hastau śūladharāyeti śūlinyai nama ityumām || 31 ||
mukhaṃ devasya saṃpūjya vāmadeveti vāstaḥ |
vāmāyai nama ityuktvā nāsāṃ caiva kapāline || 32 ||
mṛḍānyai nama ityuktvā lalāṭaṃ cendudhāriṇe |
alakāyai namaḥ paścātrinetrāya namastathā || 33 ||
tryakṣyai saṃpūjayedevīṃ śiro gaṅgādharāya ca |
kātyāyanīṃ tataḥ pūjya vyomakeśāya vai namaḥ || 34 ||
keśānsaṃpūjya vidhivatkeśinyai ca namo namaḥ |
sāmrapatre tu saṃsthāpya kalaśopāra vinyaset |
śuklavastreṇa saṃchādya saṃpūjya vidhivadvijāḥ || 36 ||
kalaśāḥ sodakā deyā brāhmaṇebhyaḥ sadakṣiṇāḥ || 37 ||
evaṃ karoti yo viprā bhaktyā'naṅgatrayodaśīm || 38 ||
prāpnoti rājyaṃ saubhāgyaṃ putrāṃśca cirajīvinaḥ |
śivalokaṃ ca saṃprāpya śaṃbhoḥ priyatamo bhavet || 39 ||
ṛṣaya ūcuḥ–
yaduktaṃ bhavatā sūta naiṣkalaṃ jñānamuttamam |
-
śrutaṃ cākhilamasmābhirmanāṃsi hṛṣitāni naḥ || 1 ||
bhaktiśca śāśvate śaṃbhau jātā'smākaṃ hi śāśvatī |
varṇāśramācāravidhimidānīṃ brūhi tattvataḥ || 2 ||
sūta uvāca -
yaduktaṃ bhānunā pūrve manave parameṣṭhine || 3 ||
yena viśveśvaraḥ śaṃbhuḥ karmayogarataiḥ sadā |
ārādhyate na cānyena ityeṣā vaidikī śrutiḥ || 4 ||
varṇāścatvāra evaite traya ādyā dvijāḥ smṛtāḥ || 5 ||
gṛhastho brahmacārī ca vānaprastho yatistathā |
na daṇḍena vinā kaśvidāśramīti nigadyate || 7 ||
brahmacārī bhaveddaṇḍī kṛṣṇājinadharaistathā |
mekhalī ca tathā muṇḍī śikhī vā yadi vā jaṭī || 8 ||
bhikṣāhāreṇa satataṃ vartanaṃ tasya suvratāḥ |
amikāryaparityāgī patitaḥ sarvakarmasu |
snātvā saṃtarpya devādīndevatābhyarcanaṃ tataḥ || 10 ||
kṛte'bhivādane kuryānnaiva pratyabhivādanam || 11 |
karoti nābhivādyo'sau yathā śūdrastathaiva saḥ |
śiṣṭānāṃ ca gṛhānnityaṃ bhikṣāmāhṛtya suvrataḥ || 14 ||
upavāsasamā bhikṣā proktā vai brahmacāriṇām |
anārogyamanāyuṣyamasvargyaṃ cātibhojanam || 16 ||
apuṇyaṃ lokavidviṣṭaṃ tasmāttatparivarjayet |
pādau prakṣālya vidhivadācamya prayato dvijaḥ || 18 ||
bhuñjīta maunī satataṃ smaredevaṃ sadāśivam |
na caiva varṣadhārābhirna tiṣṭhanpralapanna ca |
aṅguṣṭhatarjanībhyāṃ ca saṃspṛśenāsikāpuṭe |
kaniṣṭhāṅguṣṭhayogena spṛśecchratrayugaṃ dvijaḥ || 22 ||
sarvābhiraṅgulībhiśca hṛdayaṃ ca talena vā |
vinyasya dakṣiṇe karṇe brahmasūtramudaṅmukhaḥ |

śaṃbhuṃ sanatkumāraṃ ca pañcaitānpadmasaṃbhavaḥ |
na sṛṣṭau dadhire buddhiṃ śivaikadhyānatatparāḥ || 2 ||
sṛṣṭau teṣvanapekṣeṣu mohāviṣṭaḥ prajāpatiḥ |
vapastatāpa paramaṃ na kiṃcitpratyapadyata || 3 ||
gate bahutithe kāle samabhūtkrodhamūrcchitaḥ |
prāṇātmakaḥ samudbhūto lalāṭādbrahmaṇo haraḥ || 4 ||
niścakrāma tato bhittvā bhālaṃ bhagavato vidheḥ || 5 ||
rodayitvā'bjajanmānaṃ tasmādrudra iti smṛtaḥ |
anyāni sapta nāmāni śṛṇudhvaṃ munipuṃgavāḥ || 6 ||
aṣṭamī dīkṣitastatra mūrtirīśasya śūlinaḥ || 8 ||
yābhirvyāptamidaṃ viśvaṃ viśvasyāsya jaganmayaḥ |
tena viśveśvaro deva iti nāmnā śivaḥ smṛtaḥ || 9 ||
prajāḥ sṛjeti nirdiṣṭaścandramaulirviraścinā |
sasarja manasā rudrānātmatulyānmaheśvaraḥ || 10 ||
nīlakaṇṭhāṃstrinetrāṃśca jaṭāmukuṭamaṇḍitān |
vṛṣadhvajānvītarāgāñjarāmaraṇavarjitān || 11 ||
sarvajña śatakoṭīṃstānsarvānugrāhiṇaḥ parān |
dṛṣṭvā tānvividhānrudrānviraściḥ māha śaṃkaram || 12 ||
jarāmaraṇanirmuktāmīdṛśīṃ mā sṛjaḥ prajām |
sṛjasvānyāṃ sureśāna prajāṃ mṛtyusamanvitām || 13 ||
brahmāṇamabravīcchaṃbhurnāsti me tādṛśī prajā |
rudrairātmasamudbhūtaiḥ krīḍāyuktastadā'bhavat |
sthāṇuvaniścalo yasmātsthitaḥ sthāṇuriti smṛtaḥ || 15 ||
sa eva bhagavānīśo viśveśo nīlalohitaḥ || 17 ||
anugṛhya yathā māṃ tvaṃ putratve dattavānvaram || 18 ||
ardhe tatsaphalaṃ jātaṃ cintitaṃ yanyepsitam |
evaṃ viśveśvaraṃ śaṃbhuṃ samābhāṣya caturmukhaḥ ||
stotreṇānena tuṣṭāva śirasyādhāya cāñjalim || 19 ||
brahmovāca -
namaste'stu mahādeva namaste parameśvara |
svātmanyavasthitastasmai caturvaktrātmane namaḥ || 39 ||

nṛtyatyanantamahimā tasmai rudrātmane namaḥ || 42 ||

svātmanyavasthitastasmai caturvaktrātmane namaḥ || 39 ||
vibhārte śirasā nityaṃ dvisaptabhuvanātmakam |
nṛtyatyanantamahimā tasmai rudrātmane namaḥ || 42 ||
kukṣau samudrāścatvārastasmai vyomātmane namaḥ || 44 ||
tattvaṃ sadā nirākāraṃ cidrūpaṃ pārameśvaram || 46 |
prapadye paramātmānaṃ bhavantaṃ parameśvaram || 48 ||
tatastasya mahādevo nityayogamanuttamam |
yattvayā'bhyarthito brahmanputratve'haṃ mayā kṛtam |
tvamidānīṃ mamā''deśātsṛjasva vividhāḥ prajāḥ || 52 ||
tridhā bhinno'smyahaṃ brahmanbrahmaviṣṇuharākhyayā |
mamaitra hṛdayādudraḥ saṃjātaḥ kāmarūpadhṛka |
"
taṃ mahāntaṃ mahādevaṃ brahmañjānanti sūrayaḥ |
evaṃ brahmāṇamābhāṣya dattvā ca vividhānvarān || 56 ||
antarhito mahādevaḥ paśyataḥ padmajanmanaḥ |
anugrahāttatastasya tasmājjñānodayo bhavet || 57 ||
tataśca pāśavicchittiḥ śiva eva bhavettataḥ |
naśyanti vyādhayastasya galagaṇḍagrahādayaḥ || 58 ||
aihikīṃ labhate siddhiṃ ciraṃjīvitvameva ca |
sarvapāpavinirmuktaḥ śivaloke mahīyate || 59 ||

sūta uvāca -
devātha dānavāścaiva munayo manavastathā || 3 ||
nirvidyante tadā tasminsaṃjāte pratisaṃcare |
nārāyaṇo mahāyogī śete tasmiṃstamomaye || 4 ||
yoganidrāṃ samāsādya śeṣāhiśayane dvijāḥ |
udbhūtaṃ paṅkajaṃ tasya nābhau bhagavato hareḥ || 5 ||
divyagandhasamopetaṃ śatayojanavistṛtam |
'

asminekārṇave ghore śete ko'tra bhavānaho |

praviṣṭo brahmaṇo dehaṃ tatra lokāndadarśa saḥ || 13 ||
vismitaḥ kamalākānto nirgataśca vidhermukhāt |

tato viriñcirbhagavānudaraṃ kamalāpateḥ |
praviśya bhuvanānsarvāndṛṣṭvā'bhūdvismito vidhi: || 16 ||
nāpaśyannirgamaidvāraṃ pihitāni ca catriṇā |
aaisar nābhipadmasya nālamārgamavindata || 17 ||
tena mārgeṇa nirgatya brahmā brahmavidāṃ varaḥ |
reje paṅkajamadhyastho devadevaḥ pitāmahaḥ || 18 ||
tamabravīdgadāpāṇirbrahmāṇamamitardyutiḥ |
līlārthametatsakalaṃ pitāmaha kṛtaṃ mayā || 19 ||
na mātsaryātsuraśreṣṭha dvārarodho mayā kṛtaḥ |
tvameva jagato mānyaḥ sarvasyā''dyaḥ pitāmahaḥ || 20 ||
putratve tvāmahaṃ yāce dehi me kamalāsana |
padmayoniriti khyātiṃ matpriyārthaṃ gamiṣyasi || 21 ||
tataḥ svayaṃbhūrviśvādiśvakriṇe varamuttamam |
dattvā praharṣamagamatsarvabhūtātmako vibhuḥ || 22 ||
tatastamabravīdviṣṇuṃ nā॒''vābhyāṃ vidyate param ' tvanmayaṃ manmayaṃ sarvamekā mūrtirdvidhā sthitā || 23 ||
virañceyaṃ pratijñā te niṣphalaiva bhaviṣyati || 24 ||
kiṃ na paśyasi viśveśaṃ svayaṃjyotiḥ sanātanam |
sarvātmakamumākāntamanādinidhanaṃ param || 25 ||
gacchāssvābhyāṃ paraṃ devamadhikaṃ śaraṇaṃ vidhe |
evaṃ harernigaditaṃ śrutvā brahmā tamabravīt || 26||
āvābhyāmadhikaḥ kaścidvidyeteti mudhā hare |
bhāṣase nidrayāssviṣṭastyaja mohaṃ mahāmate || 27 ||

maivaṃ vidhe yadajñātvā paraṃ bhāvaṃ maheśvare |

astīti nānyathā'haṃ te bravīmi kamalāsana || 28 ||

sūta uvāca -
tvayaṃbhuvā samāviṣṭaḥ pūrva dakṣaḥ prajāpatiḥ |
prajā sṛjeti rugadī rurja ca surāsurān || 1 ||

sūta uvāca -
svayaṃbhuvā samāviṣṭaḥ pūrva dakṣaḥ prajāpatiḥ |
prajā sṛjeti rūgīdau rururja ca surāsurān || 1 ||
prajāpateraṇasya kanyāri nīti ditā |
dadau ca daśa dharmāya kaśyapāya trayodaśa |
dvai caivāṅgirase tadvadau dakṣaḥ prajāpatiḥ || 4 ||
sādhyā viśvā ca saṃkalpā muhūrtā ca hyarundhatī |
marutvatī vasurbhānurlambā jāmīti tā daśa || 5 ||
dharmasya patnayastvevāratāsāṃ saṃtatirucyate |

arundhatyāstvarundhatyāṃ marutvatyāṃ marutvataḥ || 7 ||

atha viśvādhipo rudro bhaktiṃ jñātvā dṛḍhāṃ muneḥ || 39 ||
dvīpicarmaparīdhānaṃ tripañcanayanaṃ vibhum |
taṃ dṛṣṭvā kṛtakṛtyo'bhūdupamanyurmahāmuniḥ || 41 ||
stotrainīnāvidhairdivyaistuṣṭāva parameśvaram |
tasmai prasanno bhagavāndattavānkṣīrasāgaram || 42 ||
gāṇapatyaṃ ca duṣprāpaṃ brahmādyairapi suvratāḥ |
yaha devadevena nābhūtatrāssdaro muneḥ || 43 ||
bhaktimeva virūpākṣe punaḥ punaravācata |
sarvapāpavinirmukto brahmalokaṃ sa gacchati || 46 ||
--
ṛṣaya ūcuḥ –
kathaṃ jālaṃdharo daityo nihataḥ śūlapāṇinā |
sudarśanena cakreṇa vaktumarhasi sāṃpratam || 1 ||
sūta uvāca -
āsītkṛtāntasaṃkāśī jālaṃdhara iti smṛtaḥ |
jalamaṇḍalasaṃbhūtastena devā vinirjitāḥ || 2 ||
viśvedevāstathā''dityā rudrāścaiva vinirjitāḥ || 3 ||
brahmāṇaṃ ca suraśreṣṭhaṃ samare munipuṃgavāḥ |
jagāma jetuṃ deveśaṃ viṣṇuṃ daityanivarhaṇam || 4 ||
vinirjitya tato viṣṇuṃ daityānmatīdamabravīt || 5 ||
devā vinirjitāḥ sarve varjayitvā trilocanam |
tamadya jetumicchāmi bhagavantaṃ maheśvaram || 6 ||
nandīśvareṇa sahitaṃ sāmyaṃ caiva raṇāṅgaṇe |
jālaṃdharavacaḥ śrutvā daiteyāra te dvijottamāḥ || 7 ||
yayurdevaṃ tamīśānaṃ yoddhumudyuktamānasāḥ |
tato jālaṃdharī daityo daityaiśva sahito balī || 8 ||
rathairnāgaiśca saṃnaddhaḥ prayayau śaṃkarāntikam |
dṛṣṭvā jālaṃdharaṃ śaṃbhurañjanādriyopamam || 9 ||
prahasannabravīddaityaṃ brahmaṇo varadarpitam |
yuddhenālaṃ diteḥ putra madbāṇairniśitairiha || 10 ||
kṣaṇādvicchinnasarvāṅgī mṛtyorgrāsaṃ gamiṣyasi |
kupita: māha deveśaṃ bhagavantaṃ trilocanam |
anena vāklāpena kiṃ maheśa vṛthā tava || 12 ||
gadayā tāḍayāmi tvāmanayā tīkṣṇadhārayā |
jālaṃdhara uvācaṃ- rekhāmātraṃ kimuddhartu kimidaṃ bhāṣase śiva ||17||
mervādayo'pi tiṣṭhanti kiṃ mayā na vicālitāḥ |
yā tvayā likhitā rekhā cakrarūpā maheśvara || 18 ||
tāmuddhṛtya tato hanmi tvāṃ nandipramukhaiḥ saha |
bālatve nirjito brahmā tarasaiva purā mayā || 19 ||
nikṣipto bhagavānviṣṇulīlayā śatayojanam |
indrādyā lokapālāca baddhāḥ kārāgṛhe sthitāḥ || 20 ||
dāsībhūtāḥ striyasteṣāṃ vartante mahe śiva |
dorbhyāṃ viyannadī ruddhā krīḍārtha himavadbhirau || 21 ||
diggajāśca vinikṣiptāḥ sindhau vai rāvaṇādayaḥ |
vaḍavāmukhe ruddhe caikārṇava ivābhavat || 22 ||
tasmānna jānāsi kathaṃ śaṃbho mama parākramam |
tvāmapi prāpayāmyadya jitvā kārāgṛhaṃ prati || 23 ||
netrāgnilavabhāgena camūṃ tasyādahatkṣaṇāt || 24 ||
akṣauhiṇīnāṃ sāhasraṃ līlayaiva maheśvaraḥ |
kṛtvā tadbhasmasādvimā jālaṃdharamathābravīt || 25 ||
īśvara uvāca -
samayo yaḥ kṛtaḥ pūrva lekhāmuddharaṇaṃ prati |
kuru daitya tathā śīghraṃ tato māṃ jetumarhasi || 26 ||
atha śaṃbhorvacaḥ śrutvā madāndho daityapuṃgavaḥ |
dorbhyāmāsphovya vegena lekhāmuddhartumudyataḥ || 27 ||
sudarśanākhyaṃ yaccakraṃ kṛcchreṇa mahatā dvijāḥ |
śrāvayedvā dvijānbhaktyā brahmalokaṃ sa gacchati || 33 ||
sūta uvāca -
caturṣvapi ca vedeṣu purāṇeṣu ca sarvaśaḥ |
śrīmaheśātparo devo na samāno'sti kaścana || 1 ||
brahmā viṣṇurvārātiḥ sarve yasya vaśe sthitāḥ |
utpattiḥ sarvadevānāṃ sa eva dhyeya ucyate || 2 ||
nāsti śaṃbho: paro dharmo nāstyarthaḥ śaṃkarātparaḥ |
śivādanyatsukhaṃ nāsti mokṣo naiva harātparaḥ || 3 ||
sraṣṭṛtvaṃ brahmaṇo yena dhyeyatvaṃ yena śārṅgiṇaḥ |
viṣṇutvaṃ yena śakrasya tasmādanyaḥ paro na hi || 5 ||
ṛṣaya ūcu: - kecillokā maheśānaṃ tyaktvā keśavakiṃkarāḥ |
tatra kiṃ kāraṇaṃ sūta vada saṃśayanāśaka || 6 ||
antakāle smarantyeva prāyeṇa garuḍadhvajam |
vidyamāne śive viṣṇoḥ prabhau śrīpārvatīpatau || 7 ||
caturṣvapi ca vedeṣvityādi śivanindaka kāraṇamityantamadhyā yatrayaṃ
na dṛśyate |
khasaṃjñitapustake saptatriṃśādhyāyasamāptisthale kiṃcidvahirlikhitamasti |
tadyathā - pūrvatrā dhyāyasamāptisthalavyatyāsaṃ kṛtvā'tyamadhyāyavayaṃ madhvācāryakathāparaṃ vaiṣṇavairapalapitamasti tatpusta- kāntareṣu draṣṭavyam-iti |
śaṃbhuprasāda evaipa nāsti kāryā vicāraṇā || yaḥ śaṃbhuṃ tattvato vetti sa tu nārāyaṇaḥ svayam |
yastu nārāyaṇaṃ vetti sa śakro vibudheśvaraḥ || 12 ||
ya indraṃ vetti deveśaṃ lokapālo jalādhipaḥ |
evaṃ sarvalokapālāñjānāti sa ihāmaraḥ || 13 ||
devāñjānāti yaṣṭavyāsa ṛṣirvedavitsvayam |
ṛṣīnyo vetti samyaktvātsa eva brāhmaṇottamaḥ || 14 ||
sarvadevamayaṃ vipraṃ yo jānāti sa vedavit |
rahasyaṃ vetti vedasya sa eva haravallabhaḥ || 15 ||
janmādikāraṇaṃ śaṃbhuṃ viṣṇuṃ brahmādipūrvajam |
saptadvīpapatiḥ pṛthvīprabhurekaḥ pratāpavān |
|| śūraḥ puṇyamatirbhogī dātā vedārthapālakaḥ |
tasya rājye sadā devā gṛhṇanti haviruttamam |
na pāpaṇḍī na vā bauddhastasya rājye'bhavajjanaḥ || 19 ||
kadācitsa purīṃ tyaktvā krīḍārthaṃ nirgato bahiḥ |
pṛṣṭaḥ kastvaṃ kuto yātaḥ kiṃ kāryaṃ ca tavepsitam |
kutra yāsyasi tatsarvaṃ kiṃjātīyo bhavānvada || 21 ||
kṣapaṇaka uvāca -
rājanvaṇigahaṃ śānto yatiḥ śīlavate sthitaḥ |
madīyāñcalasaṃlagnāḥ santyatra vaṇijaḥ pare || 22 ||
rājovāca -
ko dharmaḥ kiṃ nu tatra tvaṃ jñāyate kena vakti kaḥ |
kṣapaṇaka uvāca -
ahiṃsā paramo dharmastattattvaṃ yattanordamaḥ |
budhyate bauddhajainābhyāṃ vaktā tasya jino mataḥ || 24 ||
vedavedāṅgavettāro yājñikā vaiṣṇavā dvijāḥ |
māheśvarā mahāpūjyā na vyakto'haṃ bhayānnṛpa || 25 ||
sūta uvāca -
tato rājā parāṃ cintāṃ prāpto duḥkhitamānasaḥ |
dhigrājyaṃ mama durbuddhervedavāhyo'sti matpure || 26 |
evaṃ hanmi yadā pāpaṃ tadetanmāninī prajā |
kathayiṣyati śāntātmā hato rājñā kubuddhinā || 27 ||
etasminnihate kiṃ syādbhavanti bahavastathā |
dayāśabdaṃ puraskṛtya dharmo vicariṣyati || 28 ||
vedabāhyāḥ prajā rājñā śāsituṃ naiva śakyate |
tadā tatpāpabhāgī syādityāha bhagavānmanuḥ || 29 ||
sūta uvāca -
tyaktvā rājyaṃ tapastepe tato rājā pratardanaḥ |
sāvitrīṃ manasā dhyātvā nityamekāgramānasaḥ || 30 ||
brahmovāca -
putra prāpto'smi saṃtoṣaṃ varaṃ varaya suvrata |
kathaṃ tvaṃ khidyase citte rājyaṃ tyaktaṃ kutastvayā || 32 ||
rājovāca -
vedāḥ pramāṇaṃ vaktayeva jānātyeva ca yatmajā |
śaṅkāmātraṃ bhavennaiva vedaprāmāṇyagocaram || 33 ||
iti yāce varaṃ deva kimanyena vareṇa me |
yāce niṣkaṇṭakaṃ rājyaṃ saptadvīpāvanīpatiḥ || 34 ||
sūta uvāca -
evamastviti saṃmocya brahmā'ntardhānamāyayau |
pratardano'pi rājarṣiḥ saṃtuṣṭaḥ pṛthivīpatiḥ || 35 ||
tataḥ prabhṛti tadrājye sarvo dharmo vyavasthitaḥ |
agnihotrāṇi yajñāśca yatyo brahmacāriṇaḥ |
śaivā nānāvidhāḥ puṇyā vaiṣṇavāḥ śubhalakṣaṇāḥ || 37 ||
tasya rājye mahāpuṇye na pāpaṇḍī nai haitukī |
varṇāśramācāravatāṃ kriyāḥ sarvāstadā'bhavan || 38 ||
utsavā viṣṇubhaktānāṃ śivapūjā gṛhe gṛhe |
sarve devānmānayanti na kaṃcidveṣṭi mānavaḥ || 39 ||
tarkavedāntamīmāṃsāvyākhyānāni gṛhe gṛhe |
vedanirghoṣavadrājyaṃ yajñastambhāḥ sthale sthale || 40 ||
anekabhogasaṃyuktā hṛṣṭāḥ puṣṭāḥ striyaḥ satīḥ |
rakṣanti patayaḥ puṇyā yathā vṛddhapuraskṛtāḥ || 41 ||
sūta uvāca -
evaṃ bahutithe kāle. gate ye daityadānavāḥ |
pāpiṣṭhā hīnakarmāṇo mlecchāste'pi divaṃ gatāḥ || 42 ||
yeṣāṃ tu saṃtatiḥ śuddhaṃ vedamārga hi manyate |
te sarve narakānmuktvā prāptā evāmarāvatīm || 43 ||
sarvatra tulasīvṛndaṃ sarvatra haripūjanam |
vilvadalaistu sarvatra pūjyate girijāpatiḥ || 44 ||
kathaṃ tepāṃ tu pitaro narake nivasanti hi |
tasminrājye samāgatya kiṃ kuryuryamakiṃkarāḥ || 45 ||
sūta uvāca -
svargagāmiṣu sarveṣu vyāpārarahite yame || 46 ||
pūjitāḥ sarvalokeṣu sarve devā vasūvire |
tadā'sau dharmarāgatvā śakralokaṃ mahāmanāḥ || 47 ||
yama uvāca -
caturaśītilakṣāṇāṃ jīvānāṃ yā sthitiḥ sadā || 48 |
tāṃ naṣṭāmadhunā vedmi yadi devaḥ pramāṇavān |
yasyāṃ kīṭādiyonau yaḥ sthito jīvo'tipāpavān || 49 ||
narake saṃyaminyāṃ vā tatputreṇa sa uddhṛtaḥ |
śrāddhadevārcanādīni karoti śrutiniśvayaḥ || 50 ||
indra uvāca -
asmākaṃ hīnajīvānāṃ ko viśeṣo yadā śrutiḥ |
pramāṇayati tattvena vayaṃ devā yadājñayā || 51 ||
purohita tava prajñā śobhanā pratibhāti me |
pūrva cārvākavauddhādimārgāḥ saṃdarśitāstvayā || 52 ||
daityāśca dānavācaiva tathā kuru dvijottama || 53 ||
kathaṃ sā cālyate tāta na śakyaṃ hi śubhā'dhunā || 55 ||
vidhidattavarasyāhamucchettuṃ śaktimānkatham |
indrādaya ūcuḥ–
tadā śukraḥ svayaṃ teṣāṃ kṛpayā sodyamo bhavet |
tasmāsvaṃ vipraśārdūla kasmādasmānupekṣase || 57 ||
asmākaṃ durjanāḥ sarve vedakarmaratāḥ kṛtāḥ || 58 ||
teṣāṃ vyāmohanāya tvaṃ kuru yatnaṃ kṛpānidhe |
devānāṃ rakṣasāṃ caiva daityānāṃ pāpakarmaṇām || 59 ||
sūta uvāca -
evaṃ bruvatsu deveṣu vṛhaspatirudāradhīḥ |
upāyaṃ cintayāmāsa sṛṣṭeḥ saṃrakṣaṇāye ca || 60 ||
gururuvāca –
gṛṇvantu tridaśāḥ sarve mamopāyaṃ vadāmyaham |
devaḥ kaścidyadi bhavetkapaṭī vaiṣṇavaḥ svayam || 61 ||
śaṅkhacakrāṅkitatanustulasīkāṣṭhabhūṣitaḥ |
ūrdhvapuṇḍraṃ ca vibhrāṇo harināmākṣaraṃ japan || 62 ||
devatāmātranindī ca akṛtvā matimīśvare |
śivadveṣṭā mahāpāpaprerakaḥ śivanindakaḥ || 63 ||
dambhena yadi tadrājye śivanindā kṛtā bhavet |
tadā tatpūrvajāḥ sarve narakaṃ yānti dāruṇam || 64 ||
tato deveṣu sarveṣu na kaścidavadattathā |
kathayanti sma cānyonyaṃ naitatkarmāsti sundaram || 65 ||
kaścāṇḍālaḥ śivaṃ brūyātsādhāraṇyena viṣṇunā |
yasya prasādādvaikuṇṭhaḥ prāptavānīdṛśaṃ padam || 66 ||
sūta uvāca -
tataḥ kiṃnaramāhūya provācedaṃ śacīpatiḥ |
yāhi kiṃnara māyāvī bhūtvā tvaṃ vaiṣṇavo bhuvam || 67 ||
paraṃ tveko mahāviṣṇuḥ śivastasya ca kiṃkaraḥ || 70 ||
sūta uvāca -
prerito'sau balāttena bhīto'gacchacchanaiḥ śanaiḥ |
dāmbhikaṃ rūpamāsthāya yathā sādhuṃ vadejjanaḥ || 71 ||
sarvavaiṣṇavacihnāni dhṛtvā bhrābhyati tatpure |
śiṣyānkaroti tānpūrve vadenmānyo na śaṃkaraḥ || 72 ||
kacidati na dhyeyo na mukhya iti ca kvacit |
kacidutkṛṣṭajīvo'yaṃ kacicchrīviṣṇukiṃkaraḥ || 73 ||
iti nānāvidhā buddhirnarāṇāṃ bheditā yadā |
tadā śiṣyaiḥ parivṛto rājagehaṃ viśatyapi || 74 ||
cālito rājaloko'pi viruddhaṃ naiva dṛśyate |
viṣṇubhakto mahāśānto vedavedāṅgapāravān || 75 ||
upāyanānyanekāni hayāṃśca syandanānvasu |
lokāḥ sarve dadatyeva guptaṃ pāpaṃ na dṛśyate || 76 ||
sūta uvāca -
ekasminsamaye vimā ekādaśyāmupoṣitāḥ |
janāḥ prātaścakrapāṇiṃ jamatkartuṃ gatāḥ śubhāḥ || 77 ||
tatropaviṣṭaḥ śiṣyaiḥ strairvvataḥ svīyena tejasā |
na kaṃcinmanyate vipraṃ yo bhasmāṅkitabhālavān || 78 ||
etasminnantare rājā prāptavān śrīpratardanaḥ |
to bahuvidhairvibhaiḥ kuśahastaiḥ śucitrataiḥ || 79 ||
riṇaḥ kecidūrdhvapuṇḍradharāstathā |

vaiṣṇavābhāsa uvāca -
naikāgramanasaste tu ye'rcayantīha dhūrjaṭim |

rājovāca -
nānārūpāṇi rudrasya ke jānanti narādhamāḥ |
tvaṃ vaiṣṇava ivāssbhāsi vedārthe naiva vetsi re || 95 ||
sūta uvāca -
cintayitvā tato rājā viduṣo brāhmaṇottamān |
āhūya nirṇayaṃ cāsya kariṣyāmīti tattvataḥ || 96 ||1820||
trailokyanātha kimidaṃ tvayi dṛśyate'dya |

lakṣmīruvāca -
sarvātmā sarvavitkartā vaktā dhartā'vyayaḥ prabhuḥ |
tvaṃ sākṣī sarvalokānāṃ tvattaḥ parataro'rita kaḥ || 15 ||
śrīnārāyaṇa uvāca -
asti sarva varārohe mayi tattathyameva hi |
śrīmaheśava rālabdhaṃ madīyaṃ na hi kiṃcana |
|| ekaḥ sṛjati bhūtāni matsamāni kiyantyapi |
tattattvaṃ vedamyahaṃ devi madīyāḥ kecanāpare || 17 ||
vedavedāṅgavettṛṇāṃ sahasrāṇyagrajanmanām |
hananānmucyate jīvo na tu śrīśivalanāt || 18 ||
gurvaṅganāgamana kṛtsadā madyaniṣevakaḥ |
brāhmaṇasvarṇahārī ca kadācinmucyate janaḥ || 19 ||
strīghno goghno nṛpaghnaśca tathā viśvāsaghātakaḥ |
kṛtaghno nāstiko lubdhaḥ kadācinmucyate janaḥ || 20 ||
na tu śrīrudrasāmānyadarśī mucyeta bandhanāt |
virañciviṣṇuśebhyaḥ sarvotkṛṣṭaṃ na jāyate || 21 ||

svāmī madīyaḥ śrīkaṇṭharatasya dāso'smi sarvadā || 22 ||
lakṣmīruvāca_gacchāmastatra vaikuṇṭha yatra svāmyasti te vibho |
kailāsaparvate ramye praṇamāmaḥ sadāśivam || 23 ||
sūta uvāca -

tato brahmādayo devāḥ siddhāstatrā''gatā girau |

śrīmaheśa uvāca_kathayantu kathaṃ yete militāḥ sarvanirjarāḥ |

tīvraṃ khaḍgaṃ samādāya hatavānkiṃnaraṃ kudhā || 31 ||
tatpakṣapātino ye ca teṣāṃ śīrṣāṇi kaṃdharāt |
pṛthakkṛtāni paśvādyā hatā aśvā anekaśaḥ || 32 ||
na taṃ vārayate kaścidrājānaṃ puṇyacetasam |

yuyoja hayaśīrṣe taccharīrāṇi pṛthakpṛthak || 34 ||
śīrṣāṇi hayagātraiśca samyaksaṃyojya buddhimān |
uvāca vacanaṃ tathyaṃ devasaṃsadi śuddhagīḥ || 35 ||
yena vaktreṇa giriśo helitaratanmukhaṃ hayaḥ |

ghore kaliyuge prāpte mlecchairvyāptei bhuvastale |

tadā''ndhradeśamadhye tu dākṣiṇātyo bhaviṣyati |

bhaviṣyati guṇānveṣī daivādadhyayanotsukaḥ || 40 ||
padmapādukamācārya varaṃ vedāntavādinam |
advaitāgamavoddhāraṃ praṇamya prārthayiṣyati || 41 ||
ko vidhiḥ kutra vā dattā tathyaṃ śīghraṃ vadānyathā || 48 ||
bhasmasāttvāṃ kariṣyāmi hīnaṃ brāhmaṇavarcasā |
ityevaṃ kathite sarva kathayiṣyati tattvataḥ || 49||
śāpaṃ dāsyatyathā''cāryaḥ siddhānto mā sphuratvalam |
siddhānte jaḍatā te'stu paramadvaitadarśane || 50 ||
kathaṃ tvadīyā sevā me niṣphalā syādvada prabho |
ityādi vahunirvedaṃ yadā tveṣa kariṣyati || 51 ||
paścāddvadiṣyati svāmī pūrvapakṣo'stu te dṛḍhaḥ |
siddhānte sarvathaivā''ndhyaṃ mama vākyaṃ na cānyathā || 52 ||
madhunā tena śāstrāṇāṃ pūrvapakṣo vilokitaḥ |
bhaviṣyati ca vedāntamanyathā kartumudyataḥ || 53 ||
yathā yathā kaledevāḥ pracāraḥ saṃbhaviṣyati |
tathā tathā'yamunmārgaḥ śivadveṣṭarbhaviṣyati || 54 ||
pūrvaṃ tu drāviḍā deśātkarṇāṭaka tilaṅgayoḥ |
śanairgodāvarītīre prasṛto'yaṃ bhaviṣyati || 55 ||
pūrṇe kaliyuge prāpta āryāvarte caliṣyati |
māyāvādamasacchāstraṃ vadiṣyanti narādhamāḥ || 56 ||
teṣāṃ darśanamātreṇa sacaila snānamācaret |
bhadrātvaṃ ca yathā viṣṭe rāhoḥ svarbhānutā yathā || 57 ||
haritvaṃ ca yathā'neke tathaite tattvavādinaḥ |
yoganindāparā nityamagrihotrasya nindakaḥ || 58 ||
vedāntasamamityāhuḥ purāṇāni ca ye narāḥ |
kevalaṃ vepamātreṇa narā narakagāmiṇaḥ || 59||
saṃbhāṣaṇe kṛte yeṣāṃ pateca brahmavarcasaḥ |
varaṃ bauddhastathā jainaḥ kāpālika mato'pi vā || 60 ||
vyaktaṃ vadati vedānāmaprāmāṇyaṃ tu taiḥ kimu |
vedaprāmāṇyavatkṛtvā'bhimānī na ca vaidikaḥ || 61 ||
īśvaraṃ vacanādvakti paraṃ cānīśvaraḥ khalaḥ |
sūta uvāca -
evaṃ jāte tataḥ sarve yathāgatamito gatāḥ |
dehānte muktimāpannaḥ parāmadvaitalakṣaṇām |
tataḥ paraṃ bhaviṣyanti tasya śiṣyā anekaśaḥ || 63 ||
saṃnyāsiveṣamātreṇa kurvāṇā jīvikāṃ nijām |
rājasevāṃ prakurvāṇāḥ pracchannāḥ kaulikā api || 64 ||
agamyāgamane saktā abhakṣyasya ca bhakṣaṇe |
apeyaniratāḥ kecinnānābhogasamākulāḥ || 65 ||
yānārūḍhāḥ sadā rājasevāyāṃ tatparā api |
advaitanindāniratāḥ pracchannagranthagauravāḥ || 66 ||
anyadarśana siddhāntaṃ naiva jānanti tattvataḥ |
tatra doṣasya buddhayā vai paṭhiṣyanti kalau yuge || 67 ||
anyadaivatanāmāni yadi heyāni tatkatham |
vedaṃ paṭhanti pāpiṣṭhāḥ kathaṃ tarka vadanti hi || 68 ||
mīmāṃsāśāstrasadgranthānālokya ca punaḥ punaḥ |
pūrvapakṣaṃ ca sarveṣāṃ grahīṣyanti samatsarāḥ || 69 |
svakīyaṃ na vadiṣyanti yato nāsti pramākaram |
iṃsānparamahaṃsāṃ nindiṣyanti ca jārajāḥ || 70 ||
jātamātraṃ naraṃ kaṃcinmuṇḍayitvā maṭhādhipam |
kāṣāyavastramātreṇa kariṣyanti narādhamāḥ || 71 ||
māṭhāpatyaṃ ca sevā ca dhanasaṃgraha eva ca |
dāsīgamanamīrṣyā ca pañcadhā tattvavādinaḥ || 72 ||
saṃsāravattvamityeva paraṃ te tattvavādinaḥ |
māyāvilasitaṃ viśvamiti māyaikavādinaḥ || 73 ||
śuddhaṃ tattvaṃ na jānanti viśvaṃ tattvaṃ vadanti ca |
śabdamātreṇa te jātāḥ kalau hā tattvavādinaḥ || 74 ||
bhaviṣyati yadā viprāḥ pāpānāṃ mabhavaḥ kalau |
tathā tathā bhaviṣyanti chudīcyāṃ dambhavaiṣṇaṣāḥ || 75 ||
śivasāmānyavaktāraṃ śivasāmānyadarśinam |
dṛṣṭvā svāyātsacailaḥ saśivasāmānyasaṅginnam || 76 ||
madhudarśitamārgeṇa pāpiṣṭhā vaiṣṇavāḥ kalau |
bhaviṣyanti tato mlecchāḥ śūdrā yūthabahiṣkṛtāḥ || 77 ||
tasmācchṛṇudhvaṃ viprendrā māhātmyaṃ pārvatīpateḥ |
bhaktiṃ tasya sadā kartumudyatā bhavata dhruvam || 78 ||
ṛṣaya ūcuḥ -
sūta bhadraṃ samācakṣva sevako yasya mādhavaḥ |
--
śrīmaheśasya viṣṇośca tulyatvaṃ bruvate katham || 1 ||
bruvanti tulyatāṃ kecidvaiparītyena kecana |
ekatvaṃ kecidīśena keśavasya vadanti hi || 2 ||
atra siddhāntamaryādāṃ brūhi tattvena sūtaja |
avādhāṃ yena cāsmākaṃ saṃśayo vinivartate || 3 ||
sūta uvāca -
maheśānna paraṃ tattvaṃ sarvavedeṣu gīyate || 4 ||
baikuṇṭhaprabhṛtīnāṃ tu maheśakṛpayā punaḥ |
śrutismṛtipurāṇānāṃ siddhānto'yaṃ yathārthataḥ |
indropendrādayaḥ sarve maheśasyaiva kiṃkarāḥ || 6 ||
vedāntavedyamīśānaṃ pārvatīramaṇaṃ prabhum |
yo jānāti sa vaikuṇṭho duḥkhahā sarvadehinām || 7 ||
vaikuṇṭhaṃ manyate samyagīśānaṃ sa puraṃdaraḥ |
ya indraṃ manyate sarvasvāminaṃ sa ṛṣirmataḥ || 8||
svargalokaṃ samāpnoti munyājñāpratipālakaḥ |
advaitaṃ śivamīśānamajñātvā naiva mucyate || 9 ||
bhaviṣyanti narāstathyamiti dvaipāyano'bravīt || 10 ||
rudrodhāgninirdagdhe manmathe tasya bhāryayā |
ratyā vilapite tasya sakhāyo'pyatiduḥkhitāḥ || 11 ||
vasantādaya āgatya tāmūcuḥ kiṃ vidhīyate |
sarvalokeśituḥ śaṃbhorvarākā vairavā (kā) raṇe || 12 ||
ratiruvāca ---
manyeta ghātakaḥ sabaileoke'pūjyo bhavedayam |
tathā vighnaḥ prakartavyo yena kenāpi hetunā || 13 ||
asyāpakīrtirvaktavyā na caledyadi kiṃcana |
tena me duḥkhaśāntiḥ syātkiṃcinmātraṃ na cānyathā || 14 ||
vasantādaya ūcuḥ caturdaśasu vidyāsu gīyate candraśekharaḥ |
brahmā devatāḥ sarvā indropendrādayastathā |
nyūnatāṃ tasya yo brūte karmacāṇḍāla ucyate || 16 ||
tena tulyo yadā viṣṇurbrahmā vā yadi gadyate |
tulyatā yadi no śakyā nyūnatāyāstu kā kathā |
mitrasyā''nṛṇyamicchāmaḥ saṃkaṭaṃ pratibhāti naḥ || 18 ||
sūta uvāca ---
vicāthaivaṃ tadā sarve mahāmohapuraḥsarāḥ |
tapastepurmahāraudraṃ sarvalokabhayaṃkaram || 19 ||
kadācidbhagavānbrahmā prādurāsīdayānidhiḥ |
pañcamo hetuvādaśca madhunā sarva āśritāḥ |
tānuvāca tato brahmā vṛṇīdhvaṃ manasepsitam || 21 ||
yathā vāṇī ca bhavatāṃ tathā'haṃ dātumudyataḥ |
mohādyā ūcuḥ -
asmākaṃ paramaṃ mitraṃ kaṃdarpo nāśitaḥ prabho ||22||
mahādevena tenāmī ānṛṇyaṃ kartumudyatāḥ |
bhaviṣyāmo vayaṃ tāta rudrapūjābhinindakāḥ || 23 ||
yathā na labhate pūjāmasmattaścandraśekharaḥ |
brahmovāca -
adhunā na bhavedevaṃ bhaviṣyatyatha tacciram || 24 ||
bhaviṣyāma iti proktaṃ bhavatto nānyathā kvacit |
ye bhavadvaśagā lokāstebhyaḥ pūjā na dhūrjaṭeḥ || 25 ||
prārthito'yaṃ varo datto yatheṣṭaṃ kartumarhatha || 26 ||
sūta uvāca -
ityuktvā tānatho brahmā tatraivāntaradhīyata |
sarve te mantrayāṃcakruḥ kalinā saha duḥkhitāḥ || 27 ||
kaliruvāca -
bhavadbhiradhunā noktaṃ bhaviṣyāma itīritam |
tato matsamaye prāpte sarvameva bhaviṣyati || 28 ||
asmatta iti yatproktaṃ tena cāsmadvaśe sthitāḥ |
nindākarā bhaviṣyanti nāramānyo manyate na saḥ || 29 ||
lobhamohādisaṃyuktāḥ prāpte ca mayi dāruṇe |
hetuvādaṃ puraskṛtya śivabhaktiparāṅmukhāḥ || 30 ||
sūta uvāca -
tataḥ kaliyuge prāpte sarvadharmavivarjite |
mlecchairbrāhmaṇadhenūnāṃ vidhvaṃsanakare khare || 31 ||
asvādhyāyavaṣaṭkāre jaina vauddhādi saṃkule |
brāhmaṇe mlecchamārgasthe gū̃dre brāhmaṇaghātini || 32 ||
tadā vasantaḥ karṇāṭatilaṅgādikadūṣakaḥ |
madhunāmā ca vidhavākṣetre viprādbhaviṣyati || 33 ||
golakaḥ sa tu pāpiṣṭhaḥ padmapādukamīśvaram |
vedāntavyākhyānarataṃ śiṣyatvenārcayiṣyati || 34 ||
śāstraṃ pūrṇa tato'dhītya sthita āhnikavarjitaḥ |
kimagnihotraṃ ko yāgo hetumevaṃ kariṣyati || 35 ||
gururākarṇya tadvākyaṃ brāhmaṇo na bhavedayam |
iti niścitya taṃ duṣṭaṃ vakṣyati śrutatadvacāḥ || 36 ||
gururuvāca -
ko varṇastava me brūhi yathārtha vedadūṣaka |
karmabrahmodbhavadveṣṭā notpattirbrāhmaṇāttava || 37 ||
madhuruvāca—
satyaṃ vadāmi no mithyā kathaṃ māṃ paśyase guro || 38 ||
gururuvāca -
tvanmātā kena dattā re kasya putrī kadā katham |
kasmai dattā ca vidhinā kena tadbrūhi mā ciram || 39 ||
madhuruvāca -
vidhavā jananī nātha brāhmaṇena tapasvinā |
garbhiṇī samabhūttasmādayaṃ dehastato'bhavat || 40 ||
gururuvāca -
kapaṭena yataḥ śāstraṃ matto'dhītaṃ durātmanā |
tena siddhāntamaryādā kadācinmā sphuratviyam || 41 ||
madhuruvāca -
bhaviṣyati mahābhāga vacanaṃ tava nānyathā |
pūrvapakṣoṃ mama hṛdi prādurbhavatu niścalaḥ || 42 ||
-
gururuvāca —
andhatā tavaṃ siddhānte pūrvapakṣe ca pāṭavam |
bhavatyeva paraṃ tvekaṃ pāpāḥ śiṣyā bhavantu te || 43 ||
mohātsiddhāntarahitā lobhātte nṛpasevakāḥ |
krodhātkaṭhinavaktāro dambhādveṣeṇa sundarāḥ || 44 ||
hetuvādena śāstrāṇi sarvāṇi na vidanti te |
nirayeṣveva ghoreṣu gamiṣyantyacirācciram || 45 ||
sūta uvāca -
madhunāmā tataḥ prāpya śāpaṃ taṃ duṣṭabuddhimān |
bādarāyaṇasūtrāṇāṃ vyākhyānaṃ sa kariṣyati || 46 ||
madhvācāryastato bhāvādākṣiṇātyo mahānkalau |
tacchiṣyāḥ pratiśiṣyāśca nā''ryāvarte na cotkale || 47 ||
na gauḍe va ca gaṅgāyāstīre godāvarītaṭe |
mārbudāraṇyamadhye ca tatpracāro bhaviṣyati || 48 ||
yathā yathā kalervoraḥ pracāro hi bhaviṣyati |. tathā tathā mahārāṣṭra haitukā viralāḥ kvacit || 49 ||
tato'tiduṣṭasamaye mahāmlecchaistiraskṛte |
pañcavarṣastu saṃnyāsī paṭhitvā duṣṭabuddhimān |
śiṣyopaśiṣyasaṃyukto hetuvādaṃ kariṣyati || 51 ||
tattvaṃ saṃsāra ityeva na bādhyaḥ satya eva hi |
vadatyatastattvabādīḥ mithyāvādī sa ucyate || 52 ||
mithyābhūtaḥ prapañco'yaṃ mayā nirmita iṣyate |
māyāvādina ityete vastutastattvavādinaḥ || 53 ||
sacchāstraṃ jaiminīyaṃ tu karmakāṇḍapravartakam |
gautamīyaṃ tu sacchāstramīśvarapratipādakam || 54 ||
puṃsprakṛtyorvivekasya bodhakaṃ kāpilaṃ matam |
tathā vaiśeṣikaṃ śāstramīśvarapratipādakam || 55 |
pātañjalaṃ yogaśāstraṃ śaivaṃ tacchāstramiṣyate |
vedāntaśāstramūrdhanyamadvaitaṃ yacca bodhayet || 56 ||
vedāḥ sarve ṣaḍaṅgāstu purāṇānītihāsakaḥ |
smṛtiśvopapurāṇāni tathopasmṛtayaḥ śubhāḥ || 57 ||
anyonyaṃ sarvavidyānāṃ prāmāṇyamadhikārataḥ |
tātparye ca pumartheṣu sarvāṇyevaṃ jaguḥ kila || 58 |
kiṃcidvirodhe satyeva na virodho'sti tattvataḥ |
manyante śrīmaheśānaṃ sarvāṇyeva parātparam || 59 ||
pāpiṣṭhā naiva manyante vedamārga vahiṣkṛtāḥ |
ācārya madhunāmānaṃ vadanto vidhavāsutam || 60 ||
pracchanno'sau mahāduṣṭacārvāko madhusaṃjñakaḥ |
bhaviṣyati kalau viprāḥ śivanindāpravartakaḥ || 61 ||
mohātsiddhāntabāhyatvaṃ krodhācchāstraniṣedhanam |
lobhena nṛpateḥ sevā dambhādanyapratāraṇam || 62 ||
gaṇikāmaithunaṃ kāmāddhetuvādena vāditā |
bhaviṣyati kalau viprāḥ ṣoḍheyaṃ tattvavāditā || 63 ||
pañcavarṣa yatiṃ kṛtvā krameṇā''dāya bālakam |
māṭhāpatyaṃ vidhāsyanti dravyalobhena nāstikāḥ || 64 ||
pāramparya maṭhasyaiva rakṣiṣyantyabhirāgiṇaḥ |
bhogāsaktāśca pāpiṣṭhā dāsīgamanakāriṇaḥ || 65 ||
nāmnā saṃnyāsinastīrthe yānārūḍhāḥ sasevakāḥ |
naravāhanamārūḍhāḥ śikhāsūtra bahiṣkṛtāḥ || 66 ||
tatpakṣapātino mūḍhā gṛhasthāḥ śivanindakāḥ |
mithyā vaiṣṇavamānena grastā nirayagāmiṇaḥ || 67 ||
vaiṣṇavā veṣamātreṇa tantumātreṇa vāḍavāḥ |
vādinaḥ krodhamātreṇa vidvāṃso hetuvādataḥ || 68 ||
paṭhiṣyanti ca śāstrāṇi keciddūpaṇasiddhaye |
svakīyaṃ gopayiṣyanti parakīyeṇa paṇḍitāḥ || 69 ||
sūta uvāca -
mahāmohādayaḥ sarve ratimāśvāsya bhāminīm |
procuśca ślakṣṇayā vācā tadduḥ khavinivārakāḥ || 70 ||
mohādaya ūcu: - rate mā kuru saṃtāpamahaṃ mohaḥ kaleḥ sakhā |
krodhaḥ patyuḥ paro bandhurlobhamohau ca devarau || 71 ||
māpte kaliyuge pūrṇe mohalobhādayo vayam |
vasantaṃ madhunāmānamavatīrṇa ca dakṣiṇe || 72 ||
samāśritya tato hetuvādaṃ kuṭila buddhayaḥ |
kariṣyāmo yathā śakyaṃ śivapūjānivāraṇam || 73 ||
sūta uvāca -
iti te ratimāśvāsya yathāgatamito gatāḥ |
iti sarve samākhyātaṃ śivanindakakāraṇam || 74 ||
ṛṣaya ūcu: – sudarśanākhyaṃ yaccakraṃ labdhavāṃstatkathaṃ hariḥ |
-
mahādevādbhagavataḥ sūta tadvaktumarhasi || 1 ||
sūta uvāca -
devāsurāṇāmabhavatsaṅgrāmo'dbhutadarśanaḥ |
devā vinirjitā daityairviṣṇuṃ śaraṇamāgatāḥ || 2 |
bhayabhītāśca te sarve kṣatāṅgāḥ kreśitā bhṛśam || 3||
kimarthamāgatā devā vaktumarhatha sāṃpratam || 4 ||
vacaḥ śrutvā harerdevāḥ praṇamyocuḥ surottamāḥ |
hantumarhasi tāśīghramadhyānvārijekṣaṇa || 6 ||
jālaṃdharavadhārthāya yaccakraṃ śūlapāṇinaḥ |
mahādevavarāllabdhaṃ jahi tena mahābalān || 7 ||
ahaṃ devāstathā nūnaṃ kariṣyāmīti suvratāḥ || 8 ||
liṅgaṃ tatra pratiṣṭhāpya snāpya gandhodakaiḥ śubhaiḥ || 9||
tvaritākhyena rudreṇa saṃpūjya ca maheśvaram |
pratināma ca padmāni tairiṣṭvā vṛṣabhadhvajam |
bhavādyairnāmabhirbhaktyā stotuṃ samupacakrame || 11 ||
viṣṇuruvāca -
bhavaḥ śivo haro rudraḥ puṣkalo mugdhalocanaḥ |
manomayo mahāyogī sthiro brahmāṇḍa dhūrjaṭī || 19 ||
nāgacūḍaḥ sucakṣuṣyo durvāsāḥ puraśāsanaḥ || 20 ||
anādimadhyanidhano girīśo girijādhavaḥ || 21 ||
durlabho durgamo durgaḥ sarvāyudhaviśāradaḥ || 34 ||
adhyātmayoganilayaḥ sutantustantubardhanaḥ |
bhasmaśuddhikaro meruratejasvī śuddhavigrahaḥ || 200 ||
sulabhaḥ suvrataḥ śūro cāgmāyaikanidhirnidhiḥ |
unmattaveṣaḥ pracchanno jitakāmo jitapriyaḥ |
tapasvī tārako dhīmānpradhānaprabhuravyayaḥ |
adridroṇikṛtasthānaḥ pavanātmā jagatpatiḥ || 46 ||
amṛtātmetyādividhirityantaṃ kakhaga saṃjñitaputreṣu nāsti |
saṃvatsarakaro mantraḥ pratyayaḥ sarvadarśanaḥ || 48 ||
yogī yogo mahādevaḥ siddhaḥ sarvādiracyutaḥ || 49 ||
vasurvasumanāḥ satyaḥ sarvapāpaharo haraḥ |
bahuśruto vahumāyo niyatātmā'bhayodbhavaḥ || 54 ||
ojastejodyutidharo nartakaḥ sarvanāyakaḥ |
bālarūpo klonmāthī vikartā gahano guhaḥ |
dundubhī lalita viśvo bhavātmā''tmani saṃsthitaḥ || 60 ||
rājarājapriyo rāmro rājacūḍāmaṇiḥ prabhuḥ |
amoghadaṇḍo madhyastho hiraṇyoṃ brahmavarcasī |
parabrahmapado haṃsaḥ śavaro vyāghrako'nalaḥ || 65 ||
rucirvararucirvandyo vācaspatiraharpatiḥ |
ravivirocanaḥ skandaḥ śāstā vaivasvato'rjunaḥ || 66 ||
yajño yajñapatiryajvā yajñānto'moghavikramaḥ |
pañcabrahmasamutpattirviśvato vimalodayaḥ |
ātmayoniranādyantaḥ paॅṭṭrśviśo lokabhṛtkaviḥ || 75 ||
ameyo'riṣṭamathano mukundo vigatajvaraḥ |
svayaṃjyotiranujyotiracalaḥ parameśvaraḥ || 77 ||
bhago vivasvānādityo yogācāro divaspatiḥ |
udārakīrtirudyogī sadyogī sadasanmayaḥ || 79 ||
vṛhadbhujo brahmagarbho dharmadhenurdhanāgamaḥ || 82 ||
hiraṇyagarbho jyotiṣmānupendrastimirāpahaḥ || 83 ||
arogastapanādhyakṣo viśvāmitro dvijeśvaraḥ |
brahmajyotiḥ subuddhātmā bṛhajjyotiranuttamaḥ || 84 ||
jayo jarāriśamano lohitāśvastanūnapāt |
jamadagnirjalanidhirvipāko viśvakārakaḥ |
śailo nāma tarurdāho dānavārirariṃdamaḥ |
caturvedaścaturbhāvaścaturacatvaramiyaḥ |
sahasramūrdhā devendraḥ sarvaśastraprabhañjanaḥ |
padmagarbho mahāgarbho viśvagarbho vilakṣaṇaḥ || 99 ||
yajñabhugvarado devo vareśaśca mahāsvanaḥ |
devāsuragururdevaḥ śaṃkaro lokasaṃbhavaḥ || 100 ||
sarvavedamayo'cintyo devatāsatyasaṃbhavaḥ |
devādhidevo devarṣirdevāsuravarapradaḥ || 101 ||
devāsureśvaro divyo devāsuramaheśvaraḥ |
devāsurāṇāṃ varado devāsuranamaskṛtaḥ || 102 ||
sarvadevamayo'cintyo devānāmātmasaṃbhavaḥ || 103 ||
vajrahastaḥ pratiṣṭambhī viśvajñānī niśākaraḥ || 105 ||

sūta uvāca -
purā brahmādayo devā draṣṭukāmā maheśvaram |

raṇājire niśācarāstato virejurūrjitāḥ |
śṛgālavāyasāḥ paraṃ prapānamādadhuḥ
kacitparetaśāvakāḥ pratītaśoṇitā vabhuḥ || 1.20 || kvacipinākapāṇayaḥ piśācayakṣarākṣasāḥ
pratarpya cāsṛjā pitṝnsamarcayannathā''miṣaiḥ |
gajānnarāṃsturaṅgamānprabhakṣayanti nirghṛṇā-
stado paistathā'pare taranti śoṇitāpagām || 121 ||
iti pragāḍhasaṃgare surārisaṃghasaṃkule
virājate'mbikā dhanuḥ śūrāsiśūladhāriṇī |

mahārathaughaghātinī surārisainyanāśinī || 122||
tataścaṇḍikācaṇḍakodaṇḍamuktairdivāhāriṇāṃ koṭayo'ṣṭau tathā'ṣṭau |
hatāḥ paṭṭiśai rākṣasānāṃ ca lakṣāstrayastriṃśadaṣṭādaśaivātra koṭyaḥ || 123 ||
tato dānavendraṃ raṇe tarjayantī bilāsollasadvāhuvinyastaśastrā |
prameyabhāvā bhavānī mahendrādidevānmudā harṣayantī || 124 ||
hayagrīvamukhyāḥ punadaityasaṃghā daśaivāvaśiṣṭā mahāraudrarūpāḥ |
namaskṛtya raktāsuraṃ te'bhyadhāvanraṇe pārvatīṃ tāḍayanto'trapūgaiḥ || 125 ||
samuddhṛtya netrāṇi kiṃciddhasantī dviṣatsainyasaṃghāni sā saṃharantī |
nyamuñcattato'khāṇi divyāni devī nadatsvārya tūryeṣu khe'nantasattvā || 126 ||
tato girīndrajā'rīṇāṃ cakre sainyāni bhasmasāt |

duṣṭetyuktvā'tha sā devī śūlenābhihanaddhṛdi |
- saṃbhinnahṛdayo daityo mūrti cakre sudāruṇām || 130 ||

cilluñcitamūrdhāno naggrā bhūtvā vane'vasan || 134 ||

śakro'pi tāṃ praṇamyātha sarvajñāṃ viśvarūpiṇīm || 142 ||

chadmanā'pi kurute vratametaditthaṃ

nīco'pi mandamatiralpakulodbhavo'pi

sūta uvāca -
dvividhaṃ pāpamityuktaṃ prakaṭaṃ guptameva ca |
prakaṭaṃ prakaṭenaiva rahasyena tathetarat || 2 ||

samāḥ śatrau ca mitre ca hiṃsālobhavivarjitāḥ |
yaṃ brūyurukta saṃkhyākāḥ sa dharmaḥ syāditi śrutiḥ |

veśyāyā gamane caiva prājāpatyaṃ samācaret || 24 ||
nānyāsāṃ niṣkṛtirdṛṣṭā śāstreṣu paramarṣibhiḥ |
saṃvatsarasya cābhyāsādgurutalpavataṃ smṛtam || 25 ||
yadi tatra prajotpattirniṣkṛtirna vidhīyate |
śūdrā bhavati cedūḍhā brāhmaṇasya yadā tadā || 26 |
na tasyā gamane pāpaṃ prajotpattau tathaiva ca |
saṃvatsareṇa bhavati gurutalpasamo hi saḥ |
nahīṃ śailūṣikīṃ caiva rajakīṃ veṇujīvanīm || gatvā cāndrāyaṇaṃ kuryāttathā carmopajīvanīm |
dīkṣitaṃ kṣatrimaṃ hatvā caredrahmaṇo vratam || 29 ||
adīkṣitasya hanane ṣaḍabdaṃ kṛcchramācaret |
vaiśyaṃ tu kāmato hatvā tryabdakṛcchraṃ samācaret || 30 ||
caitsareṇa viśuddhaḥ syācchūdrastrīvadha eva ca |
mārjāraṃ vātha maṇḍūkaṃ śvānaṃ vai kukkuṭaṃ kharam || 33 ||
pādakṛcchraṃ careddhatvā kṛcchramaśvavadhe smṛtam |
taptakṛcchraṃ hastivadhe pārākaṃ govadhe smṛtam || 34 ||
cāṣaṃ ca śiśumāraṃ ca balokāṃ ca barka tathā || 38 ||
jagdhvā caitādvijaḥ kuryādvādaśāhamabhojanam |
nālikāṃ taṇḍulīyaṃ ca jagdhvā kṛcchraṃ samācaret || 39 ||
kāmatodumbaraṃ jagdhvā taptakṛcchraṃ samācaret |
alābuṃ kiṃśukaṃ jagdhvā prājāpatyaṃ samācaret || 40 ||
yāni kṣīrāṇyapeyāni teṣāṃ pānādvrataṃ tvidam |
prājāpatyaṃ caretsamyagretoviṇmūtrabhakṣaṇe || 42 ||
eteṣāṃ bhakṣaṇe caiva dvijaśvāndrāyaṇaṃ caret || 43 ||
kṣatriye taptakṛcchraṃ syādvaiśye caivātikṛcchrakam || 44 ||
mahāpātakinaṃ spṛṣṭvā vedavikrayiṇaṃ tathā |
rajasvalāṃ ca cāṇḍālīmajñātvā yadi bhojayet || 46 ||
tailābhyakto dvijo yastu kuryānmūtrapurīṣake || 47 ||
ahorātreṇa śuddhiḥ syācchramaśrukarmaṇi maithune |
kharayānaṃ samāruhya tathā caivoyānakam || 48 ||
nano yastu viśedāpatrirātreṇa viśudhyati |
mohādvai kurute yastu kṛcchraṃ cāndrāyaṇaṃ caret |
taraya śuddhirna dṛṣṭā'sti purāṇe munibhiḥ kṛtā |
kuryādyadi guruḥ śuddhiṃ kāruṇyātparameṣṭhinaḥ || 51 ||
śṛṇoti gurunindāṃ yastasya cāndrāyaṇatrayam || 52 |
ekāsanaṃ copaviśedguruṇā saha mūḍhadhīḥ |
prāyaścittamapīcchanti kecidajñānataḥ kṛte |
kuryātsāṃ tapanaṃ caiva cāndrāyaṇacatuṣṭayam || 54 ||
yo'yaṃ śuddhividhiḥ prokto guroraṅgīkṛtepsayā |
vāgdattasyāpradānena brahmahatyāsamaṃ bhavet || 55 ||
prāyaścittaṃ na tasyāsti dattaigrāmaśatairapi |
śivadravyāpaharaṇaṃ gurorapyaṇumātrakam || 56 ||
kutsanaṃ ca tathā śaṃbhorgurorapi tathaiva ca |
tathā ca śivabhaktānāṃ : jñānasya ca vidūṣaṇam || 57 ||
girijāyāśca viṣṇośca skandasyebhamukhasya ca |
yogināṃ ca tathā nindā nindino'pi tathā dvijāḥ || 58 ||
yadīcchecchāśvataṃ rathānamiti devo'bravīdraviḥ |
prāyaścittasya sarvasya paścāttāpo hi kāraṇam || 60 ||
na tena rahitaṃ pāpaṃ gacchatīti hi niścitam |
prāyaścitte kṛte paścāttasminpāpe pravartate || 61 ||
kṛtaṃ tvakṛtameva syāttatpāpaṃ pūrvavatsthitam |
sthūlāni yāni pāpāni sūkṣmāṇi vividhānyapi || 62 ||
tāni nāśayati kṣipraṃ muhūrta śivacintanam |
sarvapāpāpanodārthaṃ prāyaścittaṃ vadāmyaham || 63 ||
samāhito jale magnaḥ śivaṃ dhyāyanprasannadhīḥ |
aṣṭakṛtvo hara iti japanpāpaiḥ pramucyate || 64 ||
kārtikyāṃ śuklapakṣasya yā sā puṇyā caturdaśī |
japtvā'tharvaśiro yastu brahmahatyāṃ vyapohati |
tasyāmeva navamyāṃ ca bhagavantamumāpatim || 66 ||
ṛṣaya ūcuḥ -
śrutamasmābhirakhilaṃ jñānaṃ māheśvaraṃ mahat |
varṇāśramavidhiścaiva prāyacittamaśeṣataḥ |
idānīṃ śrotumicchāmo vivāhaṃ girijāpateḥ || 1 ||
sūta uvāca -
yaduvāca purā devaḥ pṛṣṭo mārtaṇḍasūnunā |
stutvā ca stotravaryeṇa tacchṛṇudhvaṃ dvijottamāḥ || 2 ||
manuruvāca -
bhagavanyadyathā pṛṣṭaṃ tattathaiva tvayoditam |
śrutaṃ tadakhilaṃ tāta hṛdi tacca sthirīkṛtam || 3 ||
jānāsi tvaṃ bhagavato māhātmyaṃ pārvatīpateḥ |
bhavato nāparaḥ kaścidvettā'stītyabravīcchrutiḥ || 4 ||
tvamīśasyāparā mūrtiryato'si parameśvaraḥ |
tvāmeva rudraṃ varadaṃ śivaṃ paramakāraṇam |
tapanaṃ śaraṇaṃ yāmi sahasrākṣaṃ hiraṇmayam || 6 ||
sūrya prabhākaraṃ bhānuṃ jyotiṣāṃ jyotiravyayam |
ambikāpatirmāśānaṃ jyotiṣmantaṃ divākaram || 7 ||
hiraṇyabāhuṃ jaṭilamoṃkārākhyaṃ pracetasam |
sūta uvāca -
evaṃ manorvacaḥ śrutvā yaduvāca divākaraḥ || 14 ||
tyaktvā dākṣaṃ śarīraṃ ca babhūvācalakanyakā || 16 ||
nāmnā kālīti vikhyātā viśvarūpā maheśvarī |
jagaccaitanyarūpa ca jaga caitanyavodhinī || 17 ||
adhiṣṭhitastayā kālyā himavānparvatottamaḥ |
puṇyasthānamabhūdvimā mokṣadaḥ sarvadehinām || 18 ||
āvāsaḥ kiṃnarāṇāṃ ca smaraṇātpupyado nṛṇām || 19 ||
śivaṃ bhartāramicchantī tasmingirivarottame |
tapastaptuṃ gatā kālī śivā pitroranujñayā || 20 ||
athāsminnantare daityattārako lokakaṇṭakaḥ |
jāto daityakule vīro mṛtyurūpo divaukasām || 21 ||
devāḥ palāyitāstena tārakeṇa balīyasā || 22 ||
devānāṃ yoṣito yāca balādapahartāśca tāḥ |
duḥkhāgninā susaṃtaptāḥ śakrādyāḥ prathitaujasaḥ || 23||
gatāḥ saśakrāḥ śaraṇaṃ brahmāṇaṃ tridaśeśvaram |

brahmovāca —
kuramācaratāḥ surā yūyamāgatā vai mamāntike |'
-
brūta tatsakalaṃ devā upāyaṃ vacmi vaḥ sphuṭam || 25 ||
devā ūcuḥ -
tārakādbhayarsa stāḥ śaraṇaṃ devamāgatāḥ |
yathā mṛtyorbhayaṃ deva tasmānnastrātumarhasi || 26 ||
api kṣaṇaṃ suraśreṣṭha na labhāmo vayaṃ sukham |
tathā'pi na jitastena devadevena cakriṇā || 28 ||
avadhyo'yamiti jñātvā yayau tyaktvā mahodadhim |
bhrāntacittastadā śārṅgaṃ gatastūrṇe mahābalaḥ || 29 ||
vayamayevamevaṃ hi bhītāstvāṃ śaraṇaṃ prabho |
āgatāstrāhi nastasmātsukhado bhava padmaja || 30 ||
brahmovāca -
śṛṇudhvaṃ me'marāḥ sarve yuṣmākaṃ sukhadaṃ mahat |
aisir earrakākhyastatāpa paramaṃ tapaḥ || 31 ||
tasya daityasya tapasā dahyamānaṃ carācaram |
dṛṣṭvā tadvaradānārthaṃ gato'haṃ tārakāntikam || 32 ||
uktaṃ mayā varaṃ vatsa varayeti mahāsuraḥ |
lāraka uvāca-
avadhyo'haṃ suraiḥ sarvairviṣṇvādyaiḥ padmasaṃbhava || bhavāmyahaṃ yathā deva tathā tvaṃ dehi me varam || 34 ||
evamastvityahaṃ tasmai varaṃ dattvā surottamāḥ |
anyaccakaṃ hitārthaṃ vaḥ kasmādvadhyo'si tadvada || 35 |
tāraka uvāca-
tasyaḥ retaḥ surāḥ pītvā garbhā viṣṇunā saha || 36 ||
bhaviṣyanti tato jātānmṛtyuriṣṭo na vā'paraḥ |
tathā'stviti tataścoktvā tato'haṃ merumūrdhani || 37 ||
na taṃ paśyāmi bho devāstārakaṃ yo vadhiṣyati || 39 ||
kathaṃ bhaviṣyatītyevamālokya manasā dvijāḥ || 40 ||
guruṇā daivataiḥ sārdhaṃ punareva sa devarāṭ |
harasyaiva sutotpattāvupāyaścintyatāṃ surāḥ || 41 ||
meroruttarattaḥ śṛṅgaṃ yatra tiṣṭhati mādhavaḥ || 42 ||
guptastiṣṭhatyameyātmā tārakādbhayapīḍitaḥ |
sabrahmakānsurāndṛṣṭvā hṛṣṭaḥ provāca mādhavaḥ || 43 ||
mādhava uvāca -
upāyaścintitaḥ ko'tra vadhārthaṃ tārakasya hi |
asti ceducyatāṃ devāḥ śarma no jāyate yathā || 44 ||
sūta uvāca -
yathoktaṃ brahmaṇā tebhyastathoktaṃ viṣṇave surai: || 45 |
kimidānīṃ tu kartavyamiti saṃcintya devarāṭ |
so'smaranmanasā kāmamajeyamasuraiḥ suraiḥ || 46 ||
śakrasya cintitaṃ jñātvā kāmo ratipatiḥ svayam |
śacīpatiṃ samāgamya prāha puṣpadhanurdharaḥ || 47 ||
koma uvāca -
kiṃ kāryaṃ tridaśaśreṣṭha kartavyaṃ kiṃ mayā prabho |
tītreṇa tapasā ko hi sthānamīheta tāvakam || 48 ||
kiṃ vā kācittavā''deśaṃ kartuṃ necchati cāṅganā |
tāṃ kāminīṃ karomyadya tava dhyānaparāyaṇām || 49 ||
na kaścidasti me śūro na mānī na ca paṇḍitaḥ |
vyāpayāmi jagatkṛtstraṃ brahmāyaṃ ratambagocaram || 50 ||
kakhaga asaṃśita pasnakeṣvayaṃ śloko nāsti |
atha kiṃ bahunoktena durvāsā vā mahāmuniḥ |
so'pi viddhaḥ patatyāśu madvāṇairmarutāṃ pate || 51 ||
indra uvāca -
jānāmyahaṃ raternātha sāmarthyaṃ puṣpadhanvinaḥ |
nūnaṃ hi sarvakāryāṇi tvattaḥ sidhyanti nānyathā || 52 ||
gaccha pārśve maheśasya surāṇāṃ hitakāmyayā |
cittaṃ harasya saṃkṣobhya pārvatyāḥ saṃgamaṃ kuru || 53 ||
etadeva hi me kāryameṣa eva manoratha: !
etasmātkāraṇātvaṃ hi smṛtaḥ puṣpadhanurdhara || 54 ||
evaṃ śakravacaḥ śrutvā valavānmakaradhvajaḥ |
madhoḥ sakhā ratīyuktaḥ pañcavāṇo manobhavaḥ || 55 ||
yatrāssste bhagavāśaṃbhunadṛṣṭyā samāhitaḥ |
niṣkampaḥ svātmanā''tmānaṃ cintayāno maheśvaraḥ || 56 ||
prāpya śaṃbhorāyatanamapaśyanmakaradhvajaḥ |
śailādi dvāradeśe tu meruśṛṅgamivoditam || 57 ||
sarvābharaṇasayuktaṃ sahasrādityavarcasam |
vajrapāṇi caturbāhuṃ dvitīyamiva śaṃkaram |
taṃ dṛṣṭvā madano vimāścintākrāntastadā'bhavat || 59 ||
kathaṃ praviśya vakṣyāmi śaṃbhuṃ tridaśavanditam |
kathaṃ kāryaṃ kariṣyāmi surāṇāṃ prītivardhanam || 60 ||
cintayitvā tu bahudhā vañcanārthāya nandinaḥ |
vāyurūpaṃ tataḥ kṛtvā sugandhaṃ mṛduśītalam || 61 ||
praviveśa tadā kāmo dakṣiṇāṃ diśamāśrayam |
tena yāmyāṃ diśi gato vāyuti sukhāvahaḥ || 62 ||
adyāpi kāraṇātso'yaṃ sugandho mṛduśītalaḥ |
apaśyattatra madanaḥ sūryakoṭimivoditam || 63 ||
sahasranayanaṃ devaṃ sahasratanumīśvaram |
nīlakaṇṭhaṃ sudhābhāsaṃ śubhrakhaṇḍendudhāriṇam || 64||
jagadutpattisaṃhārasthityanugrahakāriṇam |
jagatpādaṃ jagacchrotraṃ sūkṣmasthūlaṃ parātparam || 67 ||
rudraṃ śarva paśupatimugraṃ bhīmaṃ bhavaṃ dvijāḥ |
vyaktāvyaktaṃ trilokeśaṃ pūjitaṃ ca surāsuraiḥ |
nikṛṣya cāpamāpūrya sthitaḥ paśyanbhavodbhavam |
gatāni tasya varṣāṇi munīndrāśritajanmanaḥ |
apaśyagirijā devīmagre viśveśvaraḥ śivaḥ |
girīndraputrī tapasaḥ prasaktāṃ lajjayānvitām || 72 ||
dṛṣṭvā kimati vikalpabuddhyā kāmo'yamatreti vicintyaḥ śarvaḥ |
jñātvā vilokya pravikṛṣṭacāpaṃ netrāgnina 'sau madano'pi dagdhaḥ ||13||
sūta uvāca -
dagdhe ratipatau śaṃbhuruvācācalakanyakām |
kimahaṃ va deveśi karomi manasi sthitam || 1 ||
varaṃ brūhi mahādevi dāsyāmyadya sureśvari |
mayi prasanne deveśi kiṃ durlabhamihāsti te || 2 ||
śrī pārvatyuvāca -
te tu kāme vada nīlakaṇṭha vareṇa kiṃ deva karomi te'dya |
vinaiva kāmena na cāsti bhāvaḥ strīpuṃsayorbhāskarakoṭikalpaḥ || 3 ||
bhāvasya hāneḥ sukhasaṃnikarṣaḥ kathaṃ bhavedbrūhi sureśavandya |
uvāca bhūyo madanāntakārī dehe na cāhaṃ madanaṃ sunetre ||
netrasya caiva jvalanātmakasya svarūpametadvada kiṃ karomi || 4 ||
tadyathā - girīndra-
putra tarasaḥ prasatāṃ lajjānvitāṃ puṣpagagantakārī || iti |
tvameva dhātā jagato vidhātā viśvatomukhaḥ || 8 ||
namāmyahaṃ devavaraṃ prāṇamupendravedhomararājajuṣṭam |
śaśāṅkansūryārimayaṃ trinetraṃ dhyānādhigamyaṃ jagataḥ prakāśam || 9 ||
tvāṃ bājmayādhāramanantavīryaṃ jñānārṇavaṃ caiva guṇārṇava ca |
parāparaṃ dhāmanidhiṃ susūkṣmamanādimadhyāntavihīna rūpam || 10 ||
hiraṇyagarbha jagataḥ prasūtiṃ namāmi devaṃ hariṇāṅkacihnam |
pinākapāśāṅkuśaśūlahastaṃ kapardinaṃ meghasahasraghoṣam ||| 11 ||
tamālakaṇṭhaṃ sphaṭikāvadātaṃ namāmi śaṃbhuṃ bhuvanaika siṃham |

tvāṃ namāmi śarabharūpadharoragendrarājahāraṃ calavalayabhūṣaṇaṃ haram |

prasanno'thābravītrīṃ kālī tripurahā haraḥ |

devyuvāca -
jīvatvayaṃ mahādeva kāmo lokamatāpanaḥ |

īśvara uvāca -
bhavatvanaṅgo madanastvatpriyārthaṃ sulocane |
tena rūpeṇa lokasya kṣobhaṇāya bhavatvalam || 18 ||
jñātuṃ na śaktā brahmādyā mahatvarūpaṃ hi tattvataḥ || 10 ||
icchāśaktirahaṃ rājajñānaśaktira enaḥ |
kūṭasthamacalaṃ sūkṣma satyaṃ nirguṇamavyayam |
satpadaṃ te prapaśyanti yeṣāṃ bhaktirmayi sthirā |
nānyathā karmakāṇḍaiśca tapobhiścāpi duṣkaraiḥ || 13 ||
śivasya paramā śaktirnityā''nandamayī graham |
svecchayaivāvatāro me naiva cānyavaśātpitaḥ
tasmānmāṃ paramāṃ śaktimiti jñātvā sukhī bhava || 16 ||

evaṃ devyāḥ prasādena himavānparvateśvaraḥ |
labdhvā māheśvaraṃ jñānaṃ jīvanmuktastadā'bhavat || 18 ||
apaśyadakhilaṃ viśvamumāmaheśvarātmakam |
nityānandaṃ nirvibhāgamātmānaṃ ca tadātmakam || 12 ||
mānameyādirahitaṃ bhedābhedavivarjitam |
bāhyābhyantaranirmuktaṃ śuddhaṃ nirguṇamavyayam || 20 ||
na samīpaṃ na dūrasthaṃ na sthūlaṃ nāpi vā kṛśam |
na dīrgha nāpi vā hasvaṃ na pītaṃ nāpi lohitam || 21 ||
na nīlaṃ na ca kṛṣṇaṃ ca na śuklaṃ nāpi karburam |
pāṇipādavinirmukte na śrotraṃ na ca cākṣuṣam || 22 ||
anāsikamajihvaṃ ca manobuddhivivarjitam |
bandhamokṣavinirmuktaṃ bodhābodhavivarjitam || 23 ||
nāssdhārasthaṃ na nābhisthaṃ na hṛdisthaṃ na kaṇṭhagam |
nāpi nāsāgragaṃ viprā na bhrūmadhyagataṃ hi tat || 24 ||

morṇātantubhiṃ tattu vidyutpuñja nibhaṃ na ca || 25 ||

āsthāya paramāṃ bhaktiṃ śivayoḥ pādapaṅkaje |
pitrorhiraṇyagarbhasya śāṅgiṇaścāpi suvrata || 27 ||
sūta uvāca -
āhvānayattato viśvakarmāṇaṃ parvateśvaraḥ |
vivāhamaṇḍapaṃ kartuṃ nānāścaryavibhūṣitam || 1 ||
tenā''hṛtastataḥ śīghraṃ viśvakarmā mahāmatiḥ |
prayayau himavatpārśve kuśalo viśvakarmaṇi || 2 ||
dṛṣṭvā'tha viśvakarmāṇaṃ hṛṣṭaḥ parvatarāṭ svayam |

vidhivatpūjayitvā tu viśvakarmāṇamabravīt || 4 ||
parvata ra ḍuvāca -
yatkāraṇādihā''hūto mayā tvaṃ tadravīmyaham || 5 ||
āgamiṣyati viśveśīṃ pariṇetuṃ śivaḥ svayam || 6 ||

dṛṣṭamātreṇa sarvasya prītirbhavati vai yathā |

snāpayadvedhasā yuktaḥ sahadaiḥ śūlapāṇinam || 28 ||
strāpyamāne tadā deve nadyo vai sāgarā dvijāḥ |
babhūvuḥ salilairyuktāḥ kṛśāṅgāḥ svedasaṃyutāḥ || 29 ||
atha te tridaśāḥ sarve sanārāyaṇakā dvijāḥ |
paraṃ vismayamāpannā bhavaṃ paśyanti cādbhutam || 30 ||
tato nilīyamānāstu śarīre śaṃkarasyai tu |
nadyaḥ sarvāḥ samudrāśca prapaśyanti suvismitāḥ || 31 ||
yogamāyāhataṃ vīkṣya tattoyaṃ jagati sthitam |
tatastaistu stuto devaḥ prahasya bhagavānbhavaḥ |
visṛjya ca tadā toyamabhavatpūrvarūpadhṛt || 33 ||
evaṃ sāmye sthite tasmindevadeve pinākini |
snāpito'sau virañcādyaistrimūrtirbhagavānbhavaḥ || 34 ||
maināko'pyañjaliṃ kṛtvā devadevasya cāgrataḥ |
saṃsthito harṣasaṃyukto nidhiṃ labdhvā yathā'dhanaḥ || 35 ||
visarjitastatastena devadevena śaṃbhunā |
trailokya tila ke tasminyayau tūrṇa nagātmajaḥ || 36 ||
tadaṃśukaṃ paridhāpya devīṃ tāmarasekṣaṇām |
snāpayaṃstena kumbhena harāṅghripatitena ca || 37 ||
nīrapātaṃ dvijaśreṣṭhāḥ kṛtametatkapardinā |
pārvateyavidhirnūnaṃ kulajānāṃ sadā'naghaḥ || 38 ||
tato bhagavatī devī hṛṣṭapuṣṭā tapomayī |
piturabhyāśagā bhūtvā viveśa paramāsane || 39 ||
sūta uvāca -
athāssyāntaṃ śivaṃ dṛṣṭvā himavānparvateśvaraḥ |
merucaiva yathāsaṃkhyai ravicandradivākaraiḥ || 1 ||
vastrairnānāvidhaiśva mārgabhūṣāṃ tadā giriḥ |
patākābhirjayantībhiḥ sragdāmairdivyagandhibhiḥ || 4 ||
dhvajaiśca vividhākāraiḥ pañcavarṇairmanoramaiḥ |
cāmaraiścandraramyaistu lambakaiśca samantataḥ || 5 ||
muktānāṃ prakaraiścaiva puṣpāṇāṃ tu tathaiva ca |
evamādyairanekaiśca śobhāṃ kṛtvā nagottamaḥ || 6 ||
sthitastu vīkṣamāṇo'sau viśvavyāpinamīśvaram |
saṃpūrṇacandravadanā madanānaladīpitāḥ || 7 ||
śatakoṭyo'psarāṇāṃ tu niryayuḥ saṃmukhāśca tam |
hemapātra rāsaktāḥ padmendīvara hastakāḥ || 8 ||
maṇipātrāṇi pūrṇāni dūrvā siddhārtha kāṅkṣitaiḥ |
dadhirocanamādāya vrīhibhicampakairyavaiḥ || 9 ||
haricandanaliptāṅgā haricandana hastakāḥ |
vidumāṅkurahastāśca tathaivotpalaśekharāḥ || 10 |
cūtamañjarihastāva pārijātakarāḥ parāḥ |
svādūdakena saṃpūrṇabhṛṅgārakarapallavāḥ || 11 ||
hāvabhāvavilāsinyo madanāturavihvalāḥ |
madanāriṃ praṇemustā gāyamānāstrilocanam || 12 ||
athāsau bhagavāñśūlī cāntaryāmī maheśvaraḥ |
trailokyatilake tasminkṣaṇādāvirbabhūva ha || 13 |
tato dhanairbahuvidhaiḥ pūjayāmāsa parvataḥ |
gītaiśca vividhairvākyaiḥ praviveśa harastadā |
bhavo'bhavattadā bālodvayaṣṭavarṣākṛtiḥ svayam || 15 ||
hemāṅgo bhagavāñśaṃbhuḥ kirīṭī kuṇḍalī haraḥ |
surāsurāśca viprendrā dṛṣṭvā rūpaṃ pinākinaḥ || 16 ||
avalokya mukhā'nyonyaṃ jahasuste mudā'nvitāḥ |
āsane maje vimā nānāratnaiśca bhūṣite || 17 |
viveśa bhagavāñśūlī mahādevo jagatpatiḥ |
harasya dakṣiṇe vedhā vāmabhāge janārdanaḥ || 18 ||
śailādiragrataḥ śaṃbhoḥ kālarudraśca suvratāḥ |
rudrairgaṇeśvarairdevaiḥ siddhaiśca munibhistathā || 19 ||
upaviṣṭeṣu sarveṣu gandharvādyāḥ samantataḥ |
jagurgītaṃ ca hindolaṃ tumbarurnāradādayaḥ || 20 ||
mattamātaṅgagāminyo geyaṃ tālalayānvitam |
rambhādyapsarasaḥ sarvāḥ kiṃnaryo nanṛturdvijāḥ || 21 ||
vīṇāvallakiveṇūnāṃ mṛdaṅgānāṃ viśeṣataḥ |
dhvanibhirmanasastuṣṭirjajñe sumanasāṃ tadā || 22 ||
atha viśveśvaraḥ śaṃbhurbhūṣaṇaṃ nabhasi sthitam |
prāyacchagirijāyai tadāhlādajanakaṃ mudā || 23 ||
anenālaṃkṛtā deva mama yogyā bhaviṣyāsa |
piturdakṣasya yaḥ kopaḥ pūrvajasya varānane || 24 ||
prahāsyasi tamevā''śu bhāvaṃ caiva tu tāmasam |
tataḥ sā pārvatī devī gṛhītvā''kāśamaṇḍalāt || 25 ||
pituḥ samīpamagamadvastrābharaṇamuttamam |
mahatā hyutsavenāssśu bhūṣayitvā śivāṃ nagaḥ || 26 ||
vastrairābharaṇairdevīṃ divyairvai siṃhavāhinīm |
menotsaṅgangartā bhūyaścandralekheva toyade || 27 ||
tī nirvṛtā devī babhau tāmarasekṣaṇā |
atha devaiḥ parivṛto viṣṇvādyaistripurāntakaḥ || 28 ||
babhrāma muniśārdūlāḥ krīḍāsthānāni kṛtsnaśaḥ |
bhagavandevadeveśa viśveśāndhakasūdana || 29 ||
yā ceyaṃ paramā ramyā toyānāṃ bhrāntikāriṇī |
seyaṃ bhāti mahādeva ratnānāmīdṛśī prabhā || 32 ||
idaṃ ca dvārasaṃsthānaṃ dṛśyate lambakairvṛtam |
kuḍyasya ratnavinyāse lakṣyate dvārarūpatā || 33 ||
idaṃ citrarathākāraṃ dṛśyate vanamuttamam |
prativimbaṃ mahādeva ratnabhūmerna saṃśayaḥ || 34 ||
idaṃ ca mandirākāraṃ sopānacayamaṇḍitam |
prativimvamidaṃ caiva dṛśyate navamaṇḍitam || 35 ||
yā ceyaṃ sāgarākārā dṛśyate toyarūpiṇī |
eṣā'pi parameśāna ratnabhūmirjaloṣitā || 36 ||
yadidaṃ gaganābhāsaṃ mūrtidravyairivorjitam |
krīḍāmaṇḍapametasminpradeśe deva tiṣṭhati || 37 ||
ambarābhairmahāratnairbāhyadeśe vinirmitam |
anekavādyasaṃyuktaṃ ramaṇīyaṃ yayau haraḥ || 38 ||
evaṃ krīḍati deveśe surāsuramahoragāḥ |
dīrghikāsu taḍāgeṣu nadīṣu ca hadeṣu ca |
krīḍāvāpīṣu te ramyairtha bernānāvidhairbhṛśam || 40 ||
babhūvurdevatāḥ sarvāḥ krīḍāratiṣu lālasāḥ |
atha saṃkrīḍya viśvātmā nivṛttastatpradeśataḥ |
prāpyāssruroha prasabhaṃ sureśastadindranīlāmalavedikāntam |
sahasrapatrairvakulaiśca nāgaiḥ kīrṇa hi yatkāñcanapārijātaiḥ || 42 ||
tataḥ praviṣṭo hariṇāṅkacihnaḥ saraśmijālākula vedikāntam |
viveśa sūryāyutasuprabhāso vṛto virañcyādisuraiḥ samantāt || 43 ||
athopaviṣṭaṃ saṃvīkṣya viśveśaṃ parvateśvaraḥ |
tasya saṃsthāpya purato deveśamabravīdidam || 44 ||
himavānuvāca -
tvamevaikaḥ paraṃ dhāma ardhanārīśvarastataḥ |
devatānāṃ hitārthāya jāto hyardhatanuḥ pṛthak || 45 ||
dakṣasya duhitā devī jagaddhātrī humā satī |
vinindya ca tato dakṣaṃ tyaktvā dehaṃ nijaṃ punaḥ || 46 ||
tavaiva patnī deveśa jātā mama sutā satī |
tataḥ śrutvā girīndrasya vacastribhuvaneśvaraḥ |
prasanno varadaḥ śaṃbhurabravītparvateśvaram || 47 ||
īśvara uvāca -
jānāmyahaṃ yena mamaiva māyā śaktirvaraiṣā nagarājasiṃha |
saṃtyajya dehaṃ tava dhāni jātā yogātsvayaṃ cāruśaśāṅkavaktrā ||48 ||
ācārārthaṃ giriśreṣṭha dattāṃ gṛhṇāmi pārvatīm |
adattāṃ yadi gṛhṇāmi tathā loke'pi vartate ( varteta vai janaḥ ) || 49 ||
atha divyodakaiḥ pūrṇamādāya kalaśaṃ giriḥ |
paripūrṇasya nityasya nityānugrahakāriṇaḥ || 50 ||
prakṣālya pādau śirasā praṇamya bhṛṅgāramādāya sa śailarājaḥ |
mumoca toyaṃ bhavapāṇipaye datteti dattati tadā prajalpan || 51 ||
tato maṅgalanirghoṣaḥ samabhūtridivaukasām |
sā hārakaṇṭhī kaṭisūtradāmā subhrūlatā cāruvilolanetrā |
meroryathaivopari candralekhā tathā babhau parvatarājaputrī || 53 ||
atha vedyāṃ gato brahmā viśvamāyāṃ smarāraṇim |
dadarśodakapātreṇa vibhāvasupurasthitaḥ || 54 ||
māheśvarīṃ kāmamayīṃ dṛṣṭvā tāṃ tu pitāmahaḥ |
akṣaratsahasā śukraṃ bhagnakumbhādivodakam || 55 ||
pādena tanmamardā''śu śukraṃ tatpadmasaṃbhavaḥ |
padmajo'pi mahātejā devadevasya paśyataḥ || 56 ||
maivaṃ madeti taṃ dṛṣṭvā tripurāriḥ pitāmaham |
juhoti va (ajuhodva) canācchaṃbhorvāmenā''dāya pāṇinā || 58 ||
havanācca tataḥ prāptāḥ savitāraṃ viyadgatam |
tejomayāśca te sarve taponiṣṭhāḥ samantataḥ || 59 ||
māne tvaṅguṣṭhamātrāstu jātā hātha suvarcasaḥ || 60 ||
nispṛhā raśmipāḥ sarve sarve jvalanasaṃnibhāḥ || 61 ||
vidyādharāśvāpsarasastathā cānye surāsurāḥ |
prahṛṣṭāḥ sarva evaite pārvatyā harasaṃgamāt || 63 ||
mumoca dṛṣṭiṃ ṛturāṭsutuṣṭaḥ puṣpairanekairbhramarākulaiśca |
vādyairvicitrairvaraiśaṅkhanādaiḥ sugītagānairvaramaṅgalaiśca || 64 ||
vīṇāravairdandubhiveṇunādaiḥ samantataḥ karṇasukhaṃ prajajñe |
ānṛtyatībhiḥ surasundarībhirjegīyatībhirvarakiṃnarībhiḥ || 65 ||
daityāṅganābhiśca rasīdatībhiḥ kāmāyate'tīva tadutsarvaṃ ca ( vaśca ) |
kāñcīraveṇātha nitambinīnāṃ manobhirāmeṇa ca nūpurāṇām|| 66 ||
tāsāṃ smitenātha munīndravaryā vabhūva kāmānaladīpacaryā |
homāvasāne madhuparkayuktaṃ devāya tasmai ghubhājanaṃ ca || 67 ||
tato nivedya pramathādhipāya cakāra tuṣṭiṃ paramāṃ virañciḥ || 68 ||
atha deveṣu viśveśo varado'bhūdvijottamāḥ |
varāṃśca vividhāndattvā brahmādibhyo maheśvaraḥ || 69 ||
vyasarjayattataḥ sarvānsthāvarāñjaṅganmāṃstathā |
visarjitāḥ praṇamyeśaṃ prītiṃ te paramāṃ gatāḥ || 70 ||
evaṃ saṃkṣepato viprā vivāho girijāpateḥ |
kathito raviṇā pūrvaṃ yathāvatsamudīritaḥ || 71 ||
śṛṇoti śraddhayā yastu paṭhedvā prayatātmavān |
sarvapāpavinirmuktastejasvī priyadarśanaḥ |
sūta uvāca -
vivāhyādrisutāṃ śaṃbhuyau kailāsaparvatam |
krīḍāṃ vai varṣasāhasrīmakarottatra śaṃkaraḥ || 1 ||
gaṇairnānāvidhaiścaiva siṃhāsyaiḥ śarabhānanaiḥ |
kaiścidvayāghramukhairbhīmaiḥ kaiścidgṛdhamukhairapi || 2 ||
kaiścidgajamukhairanyaiḥ kaiścinmṛgamukhairapi |
kaiśviduṣṭṛmukhaidīyaiḥ kaiśviddhayamukhairapi || 3 ||
kaiściccitramukhairanyaiḥ kaiścidvakamukhairapi |
sūpakāsyaistathā = cānyairmārjāravadanairāpaḥ || 4 ||
sarpāsyairnakulāsyaiśca jambukāsyaistathā'paraiḥ |
śiśumāramukhaiścānyairṛkṣavaktraistathā'paraiḥ || 5 |
mayūravadanairanyairvakavaktraistathā'paraiḥ |
_ _
śākhāmṛgamukhaiścānyaiḥ kharāsyaiśca tathā'paraiḥ || 6 |
anyairasaṃkhyaiḥ pramathairjarāmaraṇavarjitaiḥ |
nityatṛptairnirātaṅgaiḥ kālasaṃharaṇakṣabhaiḥ || 7 ||
sahasrakoṭisaṃkhyākaiḥ svacchandagaticāribhiḥ |

tapasā mahatā śaṃbhuranugṛhya ca mandaram |

tatrāpi ramamāṇasya gate varṣasahasrake |
